## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-10T02:14:15.267Z

```
对照检查 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/gen/vanilla/ 的地形核心 pass 与原版 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1405/WorldGen.cs 的算法级差异（不是逐行，是用户肉眼可见的世界形态差异）：

1. **TerrainPass.ts vs 原版 TerrainPass.cs（Terarria1456 目录下找 TerrainPass.cs，1.4.5 版）**：每列地形类型的随机游走（Plateau/Hill/Dale/Mountain/Valley 概率与高度步进）、worldSurface/rockLayer 平滑是否 1:1
2. **CavesPass / 洞穴族**（原版 pass 12-15, 34: Small Holes/Dirt Caves/Rock Caves/Surface Caves/Mountain Caves）：TileRunner 参数（strength/steps 范围）、Caverer/Cavinator/CaveOpenater 调用常数
3. **Shinies 矿石**（pass 27）：六种矿的概率常数、深度三带判定、TileRunner 步数——与原版 L5837-5924 对照
4. **浮空岛/地狱/丛林/沙漠/雪原/海洋海滩**（pass 21/29/18/20/35）：岛的数量与云块结构、地狱 ash/lava 比例、丛林 mud runner 参数、沙漠沙块替换范围、海滩 TuneOceanDepth
5. **出生点/树木**（pass 78/82）：Spawn 点选择算法、AddTrees/GrowTree 的树间距与高度分布
6. ** liquids 沉降初始状态**（pass 49/97 Settle Liquids）

对每项给出：我们的实现(文件:行号+关键常数) vs 原版(行号+常数)，相同打 ✓、不同列差异点。聚焦"会导致世界长得不一样"的参数（概率/尺寸/步数/深度阈值），忽略纯重构差异。返回结构化对照表。
```

403		private static void RetargetSurfaceHistory(SurfaceHistory history, int targetX, double targetHeight)
404		{
405			for (int i = 0; i < history.Length / 2; i++)
406			{
407				if (history[history.Length - 1] <= targetHeight)
408				{
409					break;
410				}
411				for (int j = 0; j < history.Length - i * 2; j++)
412				{
413					double num = history[history.Length - j - 1];
414					num -= 1.0;
415					history[history.Length - j - 1] = num;
416					if (num <= targetHeight)
417					{
418						break;
419					}
420				}
421			}
422			for (int k = 0; k < history.Length; k++)
423			{
424				double worldSurface = history[history.Length - k - 1];
425				RetargetColumn(targetX - k, worldSurface);
426			}
427		}
428	}

1	// 原版 TerrainPass 移植(Terrarria1405/GameContent/Biomes/TerrainPass.cs,1:1)。
2	// 逐列随机走 5 种地形特征(Plateau/Hill/Dale/Mountain/Valley)驱动 worldSurface/rockLayer;
3	// 输出 worldSurfaceHigh/Low、rockLevelHigh/Low、waterLine、lavaLine 到 GenState;
4	// 海滩区按 Reset 掷的 beachLeftEnd/beachRightStart 平坦化,右海滩入口 Retarget 收平。
5	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
6	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T, W } from '../../../data/tiles';
9	
10	const enum Feature { Plateau, Hill, Dale, Mountain, Valley }
11	
12	// GenerateWorldSurfaceOffset(TerrainPass.cs:222-251,普通模式分支)
13	function genSurfaceOffset(rng: RNG, f: Feature): number {
14	  let n = 0;
15	  switch (f) {
16	    case Feature.Plateau:
17	      while (rng.next() * 7 < 1) n += rng.int(-1, 1);
18	      break;
19	    case Feature.Hill:
20	      while (rng.next() * 4 < 1) n--;
21	      while (rng.next() * 10 < 1) n++;
22	      break;
23	    case Feature.Dale:
24	      while (rng.next() * 4 < 1) n++;
25	      while (rng.next() * 10 < 1) n--;
26	      break;
27	    case Feature.Mountain:
28	      while (rng.next() * 2 < 1) n--;
29	      while (rng.next() * 6 < 1) n++;
30	      break;
31	    case Feature.Valley:
32	      while (rng.next() * 2 < 1) n++;
33	      while (rng.next() * 5 < 1) n--;
34	      break;
35	  }
36	  return n;
37	}
38	
39	export function runTerrainPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, flatBeachPadding = 5): void {
40	  const { w, h } = st;
41	  const leftBeach = gs.beachLeftEnd, rightBeach = w - gs.beachRightStart;
42	  let feature: Feature = Feature.Plateau;
43	  let num2 = h * 0.3 * (rng.int(90, 109) * 0.005);            // worldSurface(逐列游走)
44	  let num3 = (num2 + h * 0.2) * (rng.int(90, 109) * 0.01);      // rockLayer(逐列游走)
45	  let loSurf = num2, hiSurf = num2, loRock = num3, hiRock = num3;
46	  const maxSurf = h * 0.23;
47	  const history: number[] = []; // 近 500 列地表(右海滩 Retarget 用)
48	
49	  let runLen = leftBeach + flatBeachPadding;
50	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
51	    loSurf = Math.min(num2, loSurf);
52	    hiSurf = Math.max(num2, hiSurf);
53	    loRock = Math.min(num3, loRock);
54	    hiRock = Math.max(num3, hiRock);
55	    if (runLen <= 0) {
56	      feature = rng.int(0, 4);
57	      runLen = rng.int(5, 39);
58	      if (feature === Feature.Plateau) runLen *= Math.floor(rng.int(5, 29) * 0.2);
59	    }
60	    runLen--;
61	    // 出生点 ±2% 强制高原;±5% 内避免山/谷(原版换 0-2 低洼特征)

63	      feature = rng.int(0, 2);
64	    }

66	    num2 += genSurfaceOffset(rng, feature);
67	    if (x < leftBeach + flatBeachPadding || x > w - rightBeach - flatBeachPadding) {

69	    } else if (num2 < h * 0.17) { num2 = h * 0.17; runLen = 0; }
70	    else if (num2 > h * 0.26) { num2 = h * 0.26; runLen = 0; }
71	    while (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) num3 += rng.int(-2, 2);
72	    if (num3 < num2 + h * 0.06) num3++;
73	    if (num3 > num2 + h * 0.35) num3--;

77	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
78	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
79	      if (y < num2) {
80	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
81	      } else if (y < num3) {
82	        st.type[i] = T.DIRT; st.flags[i] = 1;
83	        if (y > num2 + 3) st.wall[i] = W.DIRT;
84	      } else {
85	        st.type[i] = T.STONE; st.flags[i] = 1;
86	        st.wall[i] = W.STONE;
87	      }
88	    }

90	    if (x === w - rightBeach - flatBeachPadding) {
91	      if (num2 > maxSurf) retargetSurfaceHistory(st, history, x, maxSurf);
92	      feature = Feature.Plateau;
93	      runLen = w - x;
94	    }
95	  }

97	  // 输出常量(TerrainPass.cs:109-132)
98	  const worldSurface = Math.floor(hiSurf + 25);

100	  gs.waterLine = Math.floor((rockLayer + h) / 2) + rng.int(-100, 19);
101	  gs.lavaLine = gs.waterLine + rng.int(50, 79);
102	  // num11 校正:rockLayerLow 与 worldSurfaceHigh 间距 < 20 → 拉开
103	  const num11 = 20;
104	  if (loRock < hiSurf + num11) {

106	    let spread = Math.abs(loRock - hiSurf);
107	    if (spread < num11) spread = num11;
108	    loRock = mid + spread / 2;
109	    hiSurf = mid - spread / 2;
110	  }
111	  gs.worldSurface = worldSurface;
112	  gs.rockLevel = rockLayer;
113	  gs.worldSurfaceLow = Math.floor(loSurf);
114	  gs.worldSurfaceHigh = Math.floor(hiSurf);

116	  gs.rockLevelHigh = Math.floor(hiRock);
117	}

119	// RetargetSurfaceHistory(TerrainPass.cs:256-276):将历史地表逐列下压到目标高度后重写地形
120	function retargetSurfaceHistory(st: TileStore, history: number[], targetX: number, targetHeight: number): void {
121	  for (let i1 = 0; i1 < history.length / 2 && history[history.length - 1] > targetHeight; i1++) {
122	    for (let i2 = 0; i2 < history.length - i1 * 2; i2++) {
123	      const v = history[history.length - i2 - 1] - 1;
124	      history[history.length - i2 - 1] = v;
125	      if (v <= targetHeight) break;
126	    }
127	  }
128	  for (let i = 0; i < history.length; i++) {
129	    const surf = history[history.length - i - 1];
130	    retargetColumn(st, targetX - i, surf);
131	  }
132	}
133	
134	function retargetColumn(st: TileStore, x: number, worldSurface: number): void {
135	  if (x < 0 || x >= st.w) return;
136	  for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {
137	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
138	    if (y < worldSurface) {
139	      st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
140	    } else if (!(st.type[i] === T.STONE && st.flags[i])) {
141	      st.type[i] = T.DIRT; st.flags[i] = 1;
142	    }
143	  }
144	}

142	/** Caverer(L48064)1:1:模式 0 = 主隧道+粗支洞,模式 1 = 长细链 */
143	function caverer(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, X: number, Y: number): void {
144	  const randDir = () => {
145	    let dx = rng.int(0, 99) * 0.01;
146	    let dy = 1 - dx;
147	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) dx = -dx;
148	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) dy = -dy;
149	    return [dx, dy] as [number, number];
150	  };
151	  if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
152	    // 模式 0:7-8 段,每段主挖(6-19 步,粗 4-8)+ 支挖(30-49 步,细 2-5)+ 小 runner
153	    let px = X + 0.0, py = Y + 0.0;
154	    let [dx, dy] = randDir();
155	    const segs = rng.int(7, 8);
156	    for (let k = 0; k < segs; k++) {
157	      [px, py] = digTunnel(st, rng, px, py, dx, dy, rng.int(6, 19), rng.int(4, 8));
158	      dx += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1; dy += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
159	      dx = Math.max(-1.5, Math.min(1.5, dx));
160	      dy = Math.max(-1.5, Math.min(1.5, dy));
161	      const [bx, by] = randDir();
162	      const [ex, ey] = digTunnel(st, rng, px, py, bx, by, rng.int(30, 49), rng.int(3, 5));
163	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(ex), Math.floor(ey), {
164	        strength: rng.int(10, 19), steps: rng.int(5, 9), type: -1,
165	      });
166	    }
167	  } else {
168	    // 模式 1:15-29 段长细链(湿)
169	    let px = X + 0.0, py = Y + 0.0;
170	    let [dx, dy] = randDir();
171	    const segs = rng.int(15, 29);
172	    for (let k = 0; k < segs; k++) {
173	      [px, py] = digTunnel(st, rng, px, py, dx, dy, rng.int(5, 14), rng.int(2, 5), true);
174	      dx += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1; dy += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
175	      dx = Math.max(-1.5, Math.min(1.5, dx));
176	      dy = Math.max(-1.5, Math.min(1.5, dy));
177	    }
178	  }
179	}
180	
181	function firstSolid(st: TileStore, gs: GenState, x: number): number {
182	  for (let y = 0; y < gs.worldSurfaceHigh && y < st.h; y++) {
183	    if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) return y;
184	  }
185	  return Math.floor(gs.worldSurfaceHigh);
186	}

56	export function tileRunner(
57	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
58	  i: number, j: number, o: TileRunnerOpts,
59	): void {
60	  const { strength, steps, type } = o;
61	  const addTile = o.addTile ?? false;
62	  const overRide = o.overRide ?? true;
63	  const noYChange = o.noYChange ?? false;
64	  const ignoreTileType = o.ignoreTileType ?? -1;
65	  let num1 = strength;          // 当前半径(衰减)
66	  let num2 = steps;             // 剩余步数
67	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
68	  // 原版初始速度 Next(-10,11)*0.1(0.1 步长离散);仅 speedX/speedY 任一非 0 才覆写
69	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
70	  let vy = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
71	  if ((o.speedX ?? 0) !== 0 || (o.speedY ?? 0) !== 0) {
72	    vx = o.speedX ?? 0; vy = o.speedY ?? 0;
73	  }
74	
75	  while (num1 > 0 && num2 > 0) {
76	    // 泥向上钻出世界顶即停(原版)
77	    if (py < 0 && num2 > 0 && type === MUD) num2 = 0;
78	    num1 = strength * (num2 / steps);
79	    num2--;
80	    const x0 = Math.max(1, Math.floor(px - num1 * 0.5));
81	    const x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor(px + num1 * 0.5));
82	    const y0 = Math.max(1, Math.floor(py - num1 * 0.5));
83	    const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor(py + num1 * 0.5));
84	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
85	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
86	        if (ignoreTileType >= 0 && st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === ignoreTileType && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) continue;
87	        const dist = Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py);
88	        if (dist >= strength * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) continue;
89	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
90	        // 丛林泥墙(原版 mudWall 分支:浅层墙 15,深层墙 64,均避让邻墙)
91	        if (o.mudWall && y > gs.worldSurface && st.wall[st.idx(x, y - 1)] !== 2

96	              && st.wall[st.idx(x - 1, y)] !== 64) st.wall[ti] = 15;
97	          } else if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y - 1)] !== 15 && st.wall[st.idx(x, y + 1)] !== 15

181	/**
182	 * 原版 WorldGen.digTunnel(L48132)移植:蜿蜒洞穴原语。
183	 * 速度 = (dir + 噪声游走±0.2/步) × 0.6 → 会拐弯;半径在 [Size×0.6, Size×2] 随机波动 → 粗细变化。
184	 * 这是有机洞型的来源(TileRunner strength<50 时恒速恒宽 = 直线刀砍)。
185	 */
186	export function digTunnel(
187	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG,
188	  X: number, Y: number, xDir: number, yDir: number,
189	  steps: number, size: number, wet = false,
190	): [number, number] {
191	  let x = Math.max(size + 1, Math.min(st.w - size - 1, X));
192	  let y = Math.max(size + 1, Math.min(st.h - size - 1, Y));
193	  let noiseX = 0, noiseY = 0;
194	  let radius = size;
195	  for (let step = 0; step < steps; step++) {
196	    const x0 = Math.floor(x - radius), x1 = Math.floor(x + radius);
197	    const y0 = Math.floor(y - radius), y1 = Math.floor(y + radius);
198	    for (let ix = x0; ix <= x1; ix++) {
199	      for (let iy = y0; iy <= y1; iy++) {
200	        if (Math.abs(ix - x) + Math.abs(iy - y) < radius * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.005)
201	          && st.inBounds(ix, iy)) {
202	          const i = st.idx(ix, iy);
203	          st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
204	          if (wet) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
205	        }
206	      }
207	    }
208	    radius += rng.int(-50, 50) * 0.03;
209	    if (radius < size * 0.6) radius = size * 0.6;
210	    if (radius > size * 2) radius = size * 2;
211	    noiseX += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.01;
212	    noiseY += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.01;
213	    noiseX = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, noiseX));
214	    noiseY = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, noiseY));
215	    x += (xDir + noiseX) * 0.6;
216	    y += (yDir + noiseY) * 0.6;
217	  }
218	  return [x, y];
219	}
220	
221	function inUndergroundDesert(gs: GenState, x: number, y: number): boolean {
222	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
223	  return !!ud && x >= ud.x0 && x <= ud.x1 && y >= ud.y0 && y <= ud.y1;
224	}

Caverer matches 1:1 (TS Caverer: segs rng.int(7,8) = Next(7,9) ✓, digTunnel(Next(6,20), Next(4,9)) ✓ TS int(6,19)/int(4,8) ✓; branch digTunnel(Next(30,50), Next(3,6)) → TS int(30,49)/int(3,5) ✓; TileRunner(Next(10,20), Next(5,10)) → TS int(10,19)/int(5,9) ✓. Case 1: Next(15,30) → int(15,29) ✓; digTunnel(Next(5,15), Next(2,6), true) → int(5,14)/int(2,5) ✓.

BUT: TS uses digTunnel for Dirt Layer Caves (pass 12) and Rock Layer Caves (pass 13) whereas original uses TileRunner — the TS comment acknowledges this as deliberate ("参考 1.4.4 洞型"). This is a shape difference: original dirt-layer caves are straighter TileRunner tunnels; TS makes them winding. Also the original pass 12/13 TileRunner has random initial velocity (speedX/Y 0 → random ±1.0) vs TS digTunnel uses dx,dy in [-1,1] with 0.6 multiplier — so TS tunnels are shorter per step and bendy. Also original strength 5-15 / steps 30-200 vs TS digTunnel "steps 30-199, size 3-7" — size is radius (3-7 vs strength 5-15 → radius half: strength*0.5 in TileRunner → 2.5-7.5 hmm actually similar). And pass 13: original strength 6-20 → effective radius 3-10; TS size 3-9. Roughly similar but shape differs.

Also note TS runCavesPass avoidBeach=340 for Small Holes; original uses `smallHolesBeachAvoidance` variable — need to check its value in gen setup. Let me grep. Also surfaceCavesBeachAvoidance2.

Also note TS caps the reroll loop at 40 iterations while original loops indefinitely — minor.

Also TS pass 12/13 location: pass 13 original y range rockLayerHigh..maxTilesY (exclusive), TS rng.int(gs.rockLevelHigh, h-1) ✓.

Now the remaining items. Next, let me look at Shinies original L5837-5924 full and find our shinies implementation. Search TS for shinies.

34	/** 生成一个世界。passes 按序执行，每帧尽量做完一个 pass 后让出主线程。 */
35	export async function generateWorld(cfg: GenConfig, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void): Promise<World> {
36	  const seed = hashString(cfg.seedText || String(Date.now()));
37	  const world = new World(cfg.width, cfg.height, seed, cfg.name ?? '新世界');
38	  const rng = new RNG(seed);
39	  const noise2D = createNoise2D(() => rng.next());
40	  const ctx: GenCtx = {
41	    world, store: world.store, rng, noise2D,
42	    surface: new Float32Array(cfg.width),
43	    cfg,
44	  };
45	
46	  // 原版管线:TerrainPass(五特征随机走) + TileRunner 泥石/洞穴;其余 pass 沿用
47	  // (lgcTerrain=false 走旧 fbm 地形作为回退开关)
48	  const useVanillaTerrain = (cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain !== false;
49	  const passes: Pass[] = [
50	    ...(useVanillaTerrain ? [
51	      { name: '原版地形', run: vanillaTerrain },
52	      { name: '洞穴', run: vanillaCaves },
53	      { name: '海滩', run: vanillaBeaches },
54	      { name: '生物群系', run: vanillaBiomes },
55	      { name: '清理', run: vanillaCleanup },
56	      { name: '生命水晶', run: vanillaLifeCrystals },

58	      { name: '地形', run: terrainPass },
59	      { name: '洞穴', run: cavePass },
60	    ]),
61	    { name: '矿石', run: orePass },
62	    { name: '液体', run: liquidPass },
63	    ...(useVanillaTerrain ? [

66	      { name: '半砖平滑', run: halfBrickSmoothPass },
67	      { name: '瀑布唇缘', run: waterfallLipPass },
68	    ] as Pass[] : []),
69	    { name: '地狱', run: hellPass },
70	    ...((cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain === false ? [{ name: '清浮空', run: floatCleanupPass }] : []),
71	    ...((cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain === false ? [] : [{ name: '地狱屋', run: vanillaHellHouses }]),
72	    { name: '瓦罐', run: potPass },
73	    { name: '表面', run: surfacePass },
74	    ...((cfg as GenConfig & { lgcTerrain?: boolean }).lgcTerrain === false ? [] : [{ name: '地表装饰', run: vanillaSurfaceDecor }]),
75	    ...(cfg.structures === false ? [] : [{ name: '结构', run: structurePass }]),
76	  ];
77	
78	  for (let i = 0; i < passes.length; i++) {
79	    onProgress?.(passes[i].name, i / passes.length);
80	    await nextFrame();
81	    passes[i].run(ctx, () => {});
82	    // 部分世界回调（生成期实时预览）：pass 完成即暴露，不等 finalize
83	    if (cfg.onWorldPartial) cfg.onWorldPartial(world, i, passes[i].name);
84	  }
85	
86	  finalize(ctx);
87	  onProgress?.('完成', 1);
88	  return world;
89	}

118	function vanillaTerrain({ store, rng, world, surface, cfg }: GenCtx) {
119	  const gs = newGenState(store.w, store.h);
120	  // Reset pass 掷骰(WorldGen.cs L4780-4880,掷骰顺序 = RNG 契约不可调换)
121	  // 矿石替代对:Next(2)==0 → 替代矿(内部 id)
122	  gs.oreTiers = {
123	    copper: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_tin']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_copper']!,
124	    iron: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_lead']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_iron']!,
125	    silver: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_tungsten']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_silver']!,
126	    gold: rng.next() < 0.5 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_platinum']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_gold']!,
127	  };
128	  gs.crimson = rng.next() < 0.5;
129	  // 邪恶群系覆盖（RNG 契约：骰照掷，仅覆盖结果）
130	  if (cfg.evil === 0) gs.crimson = false;
131	  else if (cfg.evil === 1) gs.crimson = true;
132	  world.crimson = gs.crimson;
133	  gs.dungeonSide = rng.next() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
134	  // 丛林:与地牢异侧(Next(15,30) 即 15-29)
135	  const jf = rng.int(15, 29) * 0.01;
136	  gs.jungleX = Math.floor(store.w * (gs.dungeonSide === -1 ? 1 - jf : jf));
137	  world.jungleX = gs.jungleX;
138	  // 地牢 X:拒绝采样直到落在地牢侧 15% 区间
139	  let dX = rng.int(0, store.w - 1);
140	  const dLo = store.w * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.60 : 0.25);
141	  const dHi = store.w * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.75 : 0.40);
142	  while (dX < dLo || dX > dHi) dX = rng.int(0, store.w - 1);
143	  gs.dungeonX = dX;
144	  // 雪原:以地牢 X 为中心向两侧扩展(L4863-4879)——原版雪原与地牢同侧
145	  const snowScale = store.w / 4200;
146	  const snowExtend = () => rng.int(50, 89) + Math.floor(rng.int(20, 39) * snowScale) + Math.floor(rng.int(20, 39) * snowScale);
147	  gs.snowOriginLeft = Math.max(0, dX - snowExtend());
148	  gs.snowOriginRight = Math.min(store.w, dX + snowExtend());
149	  // 海滩宽度(原版固定 300-340+档位加成,按 4200 宽设计;小世界线性缩放保持比例)
150	  const beachRoll = () => Math.max(20, Math.floor(rng.int(300, 339) * snowScale));
151	  gs.beachLeftEnd = beachRoll() + (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 40 : 20) * (snowScale >= 1 ? 1 : Math.max(0.3, snowScale));
152	  gs.beachRightStart = store.w - beachRoll() - (gs.dungeonSide === -1 ? 40 : 20) * (snowScale >= 1 ? 1 : Math.max(0.3, snowScale));
153	  // 地牢入口(L4891-4894):地牢侧海滩内 15% 区间随机
154	  if (gs.dungeonSide === -1) {
155	    gs.dungeonLocation = rng.int(gs.beachLeftEnd + 50, Math.floor(store.w * 0.2));
156	  } else {
157	    gs.dungeonLocation = rng.int(Math.floor(store.w * 0.8), gs.beachRightStart - 50);
158	  }
159	  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonLocation;
160	  // Terrain pass
161	  runTerrainPass(store, rng, gs);
162	  // surface 数组给后续旧 pass(surfacePass 树木等)用:每列首个实心行
163	  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
164	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
165	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
166	    }
167	  }
168	  ctxGs[0] = gs;
169	  // 世界级常量同步
170	  world.groundLevel = gs.worldSurface;
171	  world.rockLevel = gs.rockLevel;
172	  world.lavaLine = gs.lavaLine;
173	}
174	
175	function vanillaCaves({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
176	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
177	  if (!gs) return;
178	  runOceanSandPass(store, rng, gs); // 原版 pass 3(顺序上先于泥石/洞穴)
179	  runRocksAndClayPass(store, rng, gs);
180	  runCavesPass(store, rng, gs);
181	}
182	
183	function vanillaBeaches({ store, rng, surface }: GenCtx) {
184	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
185	  if (!gs) return;
186	  runBeachesPass(store, rng, gs);
187	  // 海盆挖完后刷新 surface[](后续旧 pass 依赖)
188	  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
189	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
190	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
191	    }
192	  }

195	// 原版 pass 15-22:冰原→草种→丛林→泥转丛林草→沙漠→蘑菇→大理石/花岗岩
196	function vanillaBiomes({ store, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
197	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
198	  if (!gs) return;
199	  runIceBiomePass(store, rng, gs);       // 15 Generate Ice Biome
200	  runGrassPass(store, rng, gs);          // 16 Grass
201	  const jx = runJunglePass(store, rng, gs); // JunglePass
202	  world.jungleX = jx;
203	  // 丛林表面泥化:泥柱核心区 ±0.12w 内,表层 10 格 dirt→mud(原版巨型 runner
204	  // 覆盖表面所致;我们补这一步让丛林表面成草皮而非裸土)
205	  {
206	    const core = (gs as any).jungleCoreX ?? jx;
207	    const reach = Math.floor(store.w * 0.12);
208	    for (let x = Math.max(0, core - reach); x < Math.min(store.w, core + reach); x++) {
209	      let sy = 0;
210	      while (sy < store.h - 12 && !store.flags[store.idx(x, sy)]) sy++;
211	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 10; y++) {
212	        if (store.get(x, y) === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!);
213	      }
214	    }
215	  }
216	  spreadGrassAll(store, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!, world.jungleX, Math.floor(store.w * 0.21)); // 17 Mud Caves To Grass(丛林核心区)
217	  runDesertPass(store, rng, gs);         // 18 Full Desert
218	  runFloatingIslandsPass(store, rng, gs); // 19 Floating Islands
219	  runMushroomPass(store, rng, gs);       // 20 Mushroom Patches
220	  runMarbleGranitePass(store, rng, gs);  // 21/22 Marble/Granite
221	  runDirtToMudAndSiltPass(store, rng, gs); // 23 Dirt To Mud / 24 Silt
222	  runCorruptionPass(store, rng, gs);     // 28 Corruption/Crimson
223	  runDungeonPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 30 Dungeon(蓝/粉砖主题+走廊房间+宝箱)
224	  runPyramidPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 38 Pyramids(沙岩砖金字塔+宝箱)
225	  runBuriedChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 35 Buried Chests(洞穴金/冰箱/沙箱+地狱箱)
226	  runSurfaceChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 36 Surface Chests(地表木箱)
227	  runWaterChestsPass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 39 Water Chests(水中 style17)
228	  runWetJunglePass(store, rng, gs);             // 43 Wet Jungle(丛林湿隧道灌水)
229	  runBeehivePass(store, rng, gs);               // 45 Hives(丛林蜂巢:蜂巢壳+蜂蜜+腔)
230	  runSpiderNestPass(store, rng, gs);            // 蜘蛛巢(蛛网+蜘蛛墙腔)
231	  runTemplePass(store, rng, gs, world.chests);  // 44 Jungle Temple(之字形房间链+走廊+门+宝箱)
232	  runIslandHousePass(store, rng, gs, world.chests); // 浮空岛日光板房屋(Skyware 门+箱+桌椅)
233	  runSwordShrinePass(store, rng, gs);          // 附魔剑圣地(竖井+水潭洞+土丘剑)
234	}

262	}
263	
264	// 地狱要塞 HellFort(5列×10行网格塔)——地狱层就绪后
265	function vanillaHellHouses({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
266	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
267	  if (!gs) return;
268	  runHellFortPass(store, rng, gs);
269	}
270	
271	// 生物群系感知地表装饰(替代旧 surfacePass 的种树部分)
272	function vanillaSurfaceDecor({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
273	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
274	  if (!gs) return;
275	  runSurfaceDecorPass(store, rng, gs);
276	}
277	
278	// 原版 pass 与旧 pass 的桥接存储(单次生成生命周期)
279	const ctxGs: [GenState | null] = [null];
280	
281	function nextFrame(): Promise<void> {
282	  return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
283	}
284	
285	// ---------- 地形 ----------
286	function terrainPass({ store, surface, rng, cfg, noise2D }: GenCtx) {
287	  const { w, h } = store;
288	  const base = Math.floor(h * 0.28);
289	  // 多 octave 地表高度。平坦为主、偶发高峰：
290	  // 大势噪声幂次塑形（|v|^1.8）——噪声近 0 的大片区域被压平成平原，
291	  // 只有噪声较强处才隆起成峰；小起伏幅度减半避免处处尖刺
292	  const o1 = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.012);
293	  const o2 = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.05);
294	  const o3 = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.11);
295	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
296	    const hill = (x > w * 0.18 && x < w * 0.82) ? 1 : 1.6; // 边缘更陡（近似海崖）
297	    const shape = (v: number, amp: number) => Math.sign(v) * Math.pow(Math.abs(v), 1.8) * amp;
298	    surface[x] = base + (shape(o1(x), 30) + o2(x) * 5 + o3(x) * 2) * hill;
299	  }
300	  // 左右海洋：最外侧 6% 下挖成海盆（岸边浅、世界缘深），后续灌水铺沙
301	  const oceanW = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06));
302	  const seaLevel = base + 4; // 海平面（水表高度）
303	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
304	    const edge = Math.min(x, w - 1 - x);
305	    if (edge >= oceanW) continue;
306	    const t = 1 - edge / oceanW; // 0 岸边 → 1 世界缘
307	    const floorY = base + 2 + t * 16 + Math.sin(x * 0.3) * 1.5;
308	    surface[x] = Math.max(surface[x], Math.max(seaLevel + 2, floorY));
309	  }
310	  const rockLevel = Math.floor(h * 0.33);
311	  const nBlend = createNoise1Dish(rng, 0.05);
312	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
313	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
314	    // 每列岩石线带噪声起伏（±8 格），形成犬牙交错的土石过渡带
315	    const rl = rockLevel + nBlend(x) * 8;
316	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
317	      if (y < sy) continue;
318	      let type: number = T.DIRT;
319	      if (y >= rl) type = T.STONE;
320	      else if (y > rl - 14) {
321	        // 过渡带：噪声混入石块夹层
322	        if (fbm(noise2D, x * 0.09, y * 0.09, 2) > 0.12) type = T.STONE;
323	      }
324	      store.setTileSilent(x, y, type);
325	      // 自然墙：地下土/石区背景
326	      if (y > sy + 3) store.setWallSilent(x, y, type === T.STONE ? W.STONE : W.DIRT);
327	    }
328	  }
329	  // 海洋灌水 + 沙滩铺沙：海盆内从海平面灌满水；盆底与近岸滩涂表层铺沙
330	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
331	    const edge = Math.min(x, w - 1 - x);
332	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
333	    if (edge < oceanW) {
334	      for (let y = seaLevel; y < sy; y++) store.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 1);
335	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 5 && y < store.h; y++) {
336	        const t = store.get(x, y);
337	        if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.SAND);
338	      }
339	    } else if (edge < oceanW + 8 && surface[x] >= seaLevel - 1) {
340	      // 近岸滩涂：海平面附近的陆地表面沙化过渡
341	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 4 && y < store.h; y++) {
342	        const t = store.get(x, y);
343	        if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.SAND);
344	      }
345	    }
346	    // 沙漠地下层：海洋区沙层之下换沙岩/硬化沙（5-14 格深处随机夹层）
347	    if (edge < oceanW) {
348	      const SANDSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['sandstone']!, HARDSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand']!;
349	      for (let y = sy + 5; y < sy + 14 && y < store.h; y++) {
350	        const t = store.get(x, y);
351	        if ((t === T.DIRT || t === T.STONE) && rng.next() < 0.45) {
352	          store.setTileSilent(x, y, y < sy + 9 ? SANDSTONE : HARDSAND);
353	        }
354	      }
355	    }
356	  }
357	  // 雪原带：随机一侧的 10%-24% 区间 —— 地表雪化 + 浅层冰夹层（无树：surfacePass 只在草上种树）
358	  {
359	    const ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['ice']!;
360	    const snowLeft = rng.next() < 0.5;
361	    const xs0 = snowLeft ? Math.floor(w * 0.10) : Math.floor(w * 0.76);
362	    const xs1 = snowLeft ? Math.floor(w * 0.24) : Math.floor(w * 0.90);
363	    for (let x = xs0; x <= xs1; x++) {
364	      const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
365	      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 4 && y < store.h; y++) {
366	        const t = store.get(x, y);
367	        if (t === T.GRASS || t === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.SNOW);
368	      }
369	      for (let y = sy + 4; y < sy + 12 && y < store.h; y++) {
370	        if (store.get(x, y) === T.DIRT && rng.next() < 0.45) store.setTileSilent(x, y, ICE);
371	      }
372	    }
373	  }
374	  // 世界最底部 3 行基岩（不可破坏 → 用 STONE 代替，破坏规则由硬度统一处理，简化）
375	}
376	
377	// 伪 1D 噪声：借用 2D 噪声 y=0
378	function createNoise1Dish(rng: RNG, freq: number) {
379	  const n = createNoise2D(() => rng.next());
380	  return (x: number) => n(x * freq, 0);
381	}
382	
383	// ---------- 洞穴 ----------
384	function cavePass({ store, surface, noise2D, rng, cfg }: GenCtx) {
385	  const { w, h } = store;
386	  // 1) 噪声挖空（海洋列跳过：保护海床，防止海水灌进洞穴系统）
387	  const caveOceanW = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06)) + 2;
388	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
389	    if (Math.min(x, w - 1 - x) < caveOceanW) continue;
390	    const sy = surface[x];
391	    for (let y = Math.floor(sy) + 6; y < h - 3; y++) {
392	      const depth = (y - sy) / (h - sy);
393	      const threshold = 0.30 - depth * 0.06; // 越深洞越多
394	      const v = fbm(noise2D, x * 0.028, y * 0.028, 3);
395	      if (v > threshold) store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
396	    }
397	  }
398	  // 2) 蠕虫隧道（连通大洞穴）
399	  const worms = 16 + Math.floor(rng.range(0, 8));
400	  for (let i = 0; i < worms; i++) {
401	    let wx = rng.range(w * 0.08, w * 0.92);
402	    let wy = rng.range(h * 0.35, h * 0.92);
403	    let angle = rng.range(0, Math.PI * 2);
404	    const len = rng.int(60, 200);
405	    for (let s = 0; s < len; s++) {
406	      angle += (noise2D(s * 0.05, i * 10) * 0.6 + rng.range(-0.3, 0.3));
407	      wx += Math.cos(angle) * 1.2;
408	      wy += Math.sin(angle) * 0.8;
409	      if (wx < 4 || wx > w - 5 || wy < h * 0.25 || wy > h - 6) break;
410	      const r = 2.2 + noise2D(s * 0.03, i * 5) * 1.8;
411	      carveCircle(store, wx, wy, Math.max(1.2, r));
412	    }
413	  }
414	}
415	
416	function carveCircle(store: GenCtx['store'], cx: number, cy: number, r: number) {
417	  const x0 = Math.floor(cx - r), x1 = Math.ceil(cx + r);
418	  const y0 = Math.floor(cy - r), y1 = Math.ceil(cy + r);
419	  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
420	    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
421	      if (!store.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
422	      const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
423	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r) store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
424	    }
425	  }
426	}
427	
428	function fbm(n2: (x: number, y: number) => number, x: number, y: number, oct: number): number {
429	  let v = 0, amp = 1, freq = 1, norm = 0;
430	  for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++) {
431	    v += n2(x * freq, y * freq) * amp;
432	    norm += amp;
433	    amp *= 0.5; freq *= 2;
434	  }
435	  return v / norm; // -1..1
436	}
437	
438	// ---------- 地狱层(原版 Underworld pass,L5952,完整) ----------
439	// 1) 边界随机走(h-190..h-160),界上~20格灰烬,界下清空;
440	// 2) 岩浆线随机走(h-120..h-60),线以下空气灌岩浆;
441	// 3) 大灰烬丘陵(addTile 填充虚空)+ 横向灰烬团 + 挖掘隧道(填岩浆);
442	// 4) 底部 h-145/144 岩浆层 + 地狱石 58 矿脉
443	function hellPass({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
444	  const st = store;
445	  const ASH = T.ASH;
446	  const HELLSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['ore_hellstone']!;
447	  // 1) 边界 + 灰烬带 + 界下清空
448	  let boundary = st.h - rng.int(150, 189);
449	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
450	    boundary += rng.int(-3, 3);
451	    if (boundary < st.h - 190) boundary = st.h - 190;
452	    if (boundary > st.h - 160) boundary = st.h - 160;
453	    const ashTop = boundary - 20 - rng.int(0, 2);
454	    for (let y = ashTop; y < st.h; y++) {
455	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
456	      if (y >= boundary) {
457	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
458	        st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
459	      } else {
460	        st.type[i] = ASH; st.flags[i] = 1;
461	        st.wall[i] = 1;
462	      }
463	    }
464	  }
465	  // 2) 岩浆线
466	  let lavaY = st.h - rng.int(40, 69);
467	  for (let x = 10; x < st.w - 10; x++) {
468	    lavaY += rng.int(-10, 10);
469	    if (lavaY > st.h - 60) lavaY = st.h - 60;
470	    if (lavaY < st.h - 100) lavaY = st.h - 120;
471	    for (let y = lavaY; y < st.h - 10; y++) {
472	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
473	      if (!st.flags[i]) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 2; }
474	    }
475	  }
476	  const gs = ctxGs[0]!;
477	  // 3a) 大灰烬丘陵(1/50 列):巨型竖直灰烬 runner 填充虚空
478	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
479	    if (rng.next() * 50 >= 1) continue;
480	    let y = st.h - 65;
481	    while (!st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && y > st.h - 135) y--;
482	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, rng.int(0, st.w - 1), y + rng.int(20, 49),
483	      rng.int(15, 19), 1000, ASH, true, 0, rng.int(1, 2), true);
484	  }
485	  // 3b) 每列 1/13:竖直灰烬柱 + 横向灰烬团 + 挖掘隧道(填岩浆)
486	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
487	    if (rng.next() * 13 >= 1) continue;
488	    let y = st.h - 65;
489	    while ((st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 || st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) && y > st.h - 140) y--;
490	    let scaleF = rng.int(1, 2);
491	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) scaleF *= 0.5;
492	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x, y - rng.int(2, 4), rng.int(5, 29), 1000, ASH, true, 0, rng.int(1, 2), true);
493	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
494	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x, y - rng.int(2, 4), Math.floor(rng.int(5, 14) * scaleF), Math.floor(rng.int(10, 14) * scaleF), ASH, true, 1, 0.3, false);
495	    }
496	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
497	      const s2 = rng.int(1, 2);
498	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x, y - rng.int(2, 4), Math.floor(rng.int(5, 14) * s2), Math.floor(rng.int(10, 14) * s2), ASH, true, -1, 0.3, false);
499	    }
500	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x + rng.int(-10, 9), y + rng.int(-10, 10), rng.int(5, 14), rng.int(5, 9), -2, false, rng.int(-1, 2), rng.int(-1, 2), false);
501	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) {
502	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x + rng.int(-10, 9), y + rng.int(-10, 10), rng.int(10, 29), rng.int(10, 19), -2, false, rng.int(-1, 2), rng.int(-1, 2), false);
503	    }
504	    if (rng.next() * 5 < 1) {
505	      tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, x + rng.int(-15, 14), y + rng.int(-15, 9), rng.int(15, 29), rng.int(5, 19), -2, false, rng.int(-1, 2), rng.int(-1, 2), false);
506	    }
507	  }
508	  // 3c) 随机小挖掘(w 个)
509	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w; n++) {
510	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, rng.int(20, st.w - 21), rng.int(st.h - 180, st.h - 11),
511	      rng.int(2, 6), rng.int(2, 6), -2, false, 0, 0, false);
512	  }
513	  // 4) 底部岩浆层(h-145/144)+ 地狱石脉
514	  for (let x = 0; x < st.w; x++) {
515	    for (const dy of [145, 144]) {
516	      const i = st.idx(x, st.h - dy);
517	      if (!st.flags[i]) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 2; }
518	    }
519	  }
520	  for (let n = 0; n < st.w * st.h * 0.0008; n++) {
521	    tileRunnerHell(st, rng, gs, rng.int(0, st.w - 1), rng.int(st.h - 140, st.h - 1),
522	      rng.int(2, 6), rng.int(3, 6), HELLSTONE, false, 0, 0, false);
523	  }
524	}
525	
526	/** 地狱专用 runner:超界安全(地狱区常越界) */
527	function tileRunnerHell(st: World['store'], rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number,
528	  strength: number, steps: number, type: number, addTile: boolean,
529	  speedX: number, speedY: number, noYChange: boolean): void {
530	  tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i, Math.max(2, Math.min(st.h - 3, j)), {
531	    strength, steps, type, addTile, speedX, speedY, noYChange,
532	  });
533	}
534	
535	// ---------- 浮空方块清理 ----------
536	// 迭代剥离无支撑的孤立土/石块（下方三格全空且左右也空 → 移除），重复数次处理小簇
537	function floatCleanupPass({ store, surface }: GenCtx) {
538	  const st = store;
539	  for (let iter = 0; iter < 4; iter++) {
540	    let removed = 0;
541	    for (let x = 2; x < st.w - 2; x++) {
542	      const surfY = Math.floor(surface[x]);
543	      for (let y = surfY + 2; y < st.h - 2; y++) {
544	        const t = st.get(x, y);
545	        if (t !== T.DIRT && t !== T.STONE && t !== T.GRASS) continue;
546	        const below = st.isSolid(x, y + 1) || st.isSolid(x - 1, y + 1) || st.isSolid(x + 1, y + 1);
547	        const beside = st.isSolid(x - 1, y) || st.isSolid(x + 1, y);
548	        const above = st.isSolid(x, y - 1);
549	        if (!below && !beside && !above) {
550	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
551	          removed++;
552	        }
553	      }
554	    }
555	    if (removed === 0) break;
556	  }
557	}
558	
559	// ---------- 瓦罐散布 ----------
560	function potPass({ store, surface, rng }: GenCtx) {
561	  const { w, h } = store;
562	  const POT = TILE_BY_KEY['pot']!;
563	  // 尝试次数随世界宽度；洞穴/浅层洞的地面（下方实心、上方 2x2 净空）放 1-3 个一组
564	  const tries = Math.floor(w / 6);
565	  for (let i = 0; i < tries; i++) {
566	    const x = rng.int(4, w - 5);
567	    const yMin = Math.floor(surface[x]) + 8;
568	    const yMax = Math.min(h - 6, Math.floor(surface[x]) + Math.floor(h * 0.45));
569	    if (yMax <= yMin) continue;
570	    const y = rng.int(yMin, yMax);
571	    // 2x2 净空 + 底部实心 = 洞内地面
572	    let ok = true;
573	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2 && ok; dx++) {
574	      for (let dy = 1; dy <= 2; dy++) {
575	        if (store.isActive(x + dx, y - dy)) { ok = false; break; }
576	      }
577	    }
578	    if (!ok || !store.isSolid(x, y) || !store.isSolid(x + 1, y)) continue;
579	    // 该点 12 格内有其他瓦罐则跳过（避免扎堆）
580	    let nearby = false;
581	    for (let dx = -6; dx <= 6 && !nearby; dx++) {
582	      for (let dy = -4; dy <= 2; dy++) {
583	        if (store.get(x + dx, y + dy) === POT) { nearby = true; break; }
584	      }
585	    }
586	    if (nearby) continue;
587	    // 放 1-3 个（间距 2-3 格，各自需独立净空）
588	    const n = rng.int(1, 3);
589	    let px = x;
590	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
591	      if (store.get(px, y - 1) === 0 && store.get(px + 1, y - 1) === 0 &&
592	          store.get(px, y - 2) === 0 && store.get(px + 1, y - 2) === 0 && store.isSolid(px, y)) {
593	        store.setTileSilent(px, y - 1, POT, 0, 0);
594	        store.setTileSilent(px + 1, y - 1, POT, 18, 0);
595	        store.setTileSilent(px, y - 2, POT, 0, 18);
596	        store.setTileSilent(px + 1, y - 2, POT, 18, 18);
597	      }
598	      px += rng.int(2, 4);
599	      if (px > w - 4) break;
600	    }
601	  }
602	}
603	
604	// ---------- 矿石 ----------
605	function orePass({ store, surface, rng, cfg }: GenCtx) {
606	  const { w, h } = store;
607	  // 替代矿对（按种子二选一，同原版每世界固定一种）
608	  const pair = (a: string, b: string) => TILE_BY_KEY[rng.next() < 0.5 ? a : b]!;
609	  const ores: Array<{ type: number; depthMin: number; depthMax: number; tries: number; size: [number, number] }> = [
610	    { type: pair('ore_copper', 'ore_tin'), depthMin: 0.02, depthMax: 0.45, tries: 340, size: [4, 10] },
611	    { type: pair('ore_iron', 'ore_lead'), depthMin: 0.10, depthMax: 0.62, tries: 300, size: [4, 9] },
612	    { type: pair('ore_silver', 'ore_tungsten'), depthMin: 0.30, depthMax: 0.85, tries: 220, size: [3, 7] },
613	    { type: pair('ore_gold', 'ore_platinum'), depthMin: 0.50, depthMax: 1.0, tries: 160, size: [3, 7] },
614	    // 宝石脉：小而散，全深度零星分布
615	    ...['gem_sapphire', 'gem_ruby', 'gem_emerald', 'gem_topaz', 'gem_amethyst', 'gem_diamond']
616	      .map((k) => ({ type: TILE_BY_KEY[k]!, depthMin: 0.15, depthMax: 0.95, tries: 30, size: [3, 6] as [number, number] })),
617	    // 粘土/淤泥团（浅层大块）
618	    { type: TILE_BY_KEY['clay']!, depthMin: 0.03, depthMax: 0.3, tries: 60, size: [8, 16] },
619	    { type: TILE_BY_KEY['silt']!, depthMin: 0.25, depthMax: 0.6, tries: 40, size: [6, 12] },
620	  ];
621	  for (const ore of ores) {
622	    for (let i = 0; i < ore.tries; i++) {
623	      const x = rng.int(2, w - 3);
624	      const yMin = Math.floor(surface[x] + 6 + (h - surface[x]) * ore.depthMin);
625	      const yMax = Math.min(h - 4, Math.floor(surface[x] + 6 + (h - surface[x]) * ore.depthMax));
626	      if (yMax <= yMin) continue;
627	      const y = rng.int(yMin, yMax);
628	      growOreBlob(store, x, y, ore.type, rng.int(ore.size[0], ore.size[1]), rng);
629	    }
630	  }
631	}
632	
633	function growOreBlob(store: GenCtx['store'], x: number, y: number, type: number, size: number, rng: RNG) {
634	  let cx = x, cy = y;
635	  for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) {
636	    if (store.inBounds(cx, cy)) {
637	      const t = store.type[store.idx(cx, cy)];
638	      if (t === T.STONE || t === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(cx, cy, type);
639	    }
640	    cx += rng.int(-1, 1);
641	    cy += rng.int(-1, 1);
642	  }
643	}
644	
645	// ---------- 液体 ----------
646	function liquidPass({ store, surface, rng }: GenCtx) {
647	  const { w, h } = store;
648	  // 地表洼地灌水（边缘海洋区已有水，跳过避免重复判定）
649	  const lakeOceanW = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06)) + 6;
650	  let x = lakeOceanW;
651	  while (x < w - lakeOceanW) {
652	    const sy = surface[x];
653	    // 局部极小值
654	    if (sy > surface[x - 3] + 2 && sy > surface[x + 3] + 2) {
655	      const lakeW = rng.int(10, 26);
656	      // 找洼地范围内的最高岸
657	      let basinFloor = sy;
658	      for (let dx = 0; dx < lakeW; dx++) basinFloor = Math.max(basinFloor, surface[Math.min(w - 1, x + dx)]);
659	      const waterTop = Math.max(sy - 2, basinFloor - rng.int(2, 5));
660	      for (let dx = 0; dx < lakeW && x + dx < w; dx++) {
661	        // 逐列只填到该列自己的地表之上（waterTop < y < 该列 surface），
662	        // 绝不灌进实心地面内部
663	        const colFloor = surface[x + dx];
664	        for (let y = Math.floor(waterTop); y < Math.floor(colFloor); y++) {
665	          if (!store.inBounds(x + dx, y)) continue;
666	          // 只清非实心内容（杂草等），不动实心块
667	          const t = store.get(x + dx, y);
668	          if (t !== 0 && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid) store.setTileSilent(x + dx, y, 0);
669	          if (t === 0 || !TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid) store.setLiquid(x + dx, y, 255, 1);
670	        }
671	      }
672	      x += lakeW + 6;
673	    } else {
674	      x++;
675	    }
676	  }
677	  // 地下洞穴低处静水
678	  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
679	    const lx = rng.int(10, w - 10);
680	    const ly = rng.int(Math.floor(h * 0.5), h - 30);
681	    const poolW = rng.int(5, 14), poolH = rng.int(2, 4);
682	    for (let dx = 0; dx < poolW; dx++) {
683	      for (let dy = 0; dy < poolH; dy++) {
684	        const px = lx + dx, py = ly + dy;
685	        if (!store.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
686	        if (!store.isActive(px, py) && store.isSolid(px, py + 1)) {
687	          store.setLiquid(px, py, 255, 1);
688	        }
689	      }
690	    }
691	  }
692	}
693	
694	// ---------- 表面 ----------
695	function surfacePass({ store, surface, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
696	  const { w } = store;
697	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
698	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
699	    if (store.get(x, sy) === T.DIRT) {
700	      store.setTileSilent(x, sy, T.GRASS);
701	    }
702	    // 装饰：草丛/花/蘑菇——放在草块上方空气格（sy 是首个实心行，装饰在 sy-1）
703	    // 帧为 Tiles_3 表的像素偏移（TEdit frames 数据）：草丛 6 变体 / 花 9 色 / 蘑菇 (144,0)
704	    if (sy - 1 >= 0 && !store.isActive(x, sy - 1) && store.isSolid(x, sy)) {
705	      const GRASS_FX = [0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90];
706	      const FLOWER_FX = [108, 126, 162, 180, 216, 252, 342, 540, 594];
707	      const r = rng.next();
708	      if (r < 0.34) store.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.TALLGRASS, GRASS_FX[Math.floor(rng.next() * GRASS_FX.length) % 6], 0);
709	      else if (r < 0.48) {
710	        store.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.FLOWER, FLOWER_FX[Math.floor(rng.next() * FLOWER_FX.length) % 9], 0);
711	        // 花簇：50% 概率向两侧蔓延 1 格（同簇同帧）
712	        const fx = store.frameX[store.idx(x, sy - 1)];
713	        for (const dx of [-1, 1]) {
714	          if (rng.next() < 0.5 && store.get(x + dx, sy - 1) === 0 && store.isSolid(x + dx, sy)) {
715	            store.setTileSilent(x + dx, sy - 1, T.FLOWER, fx, 0);
716	          }
717	        }
718	      }
719	      else if (r < 0.51) store.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.MUSHROOM, 144, 0);
720	    }
721	  }
722	  // 树：正常间距 6-14；连续 24 格无树则放宽条件强制补种，保证顶部林区连续
723	  let x = rng.int(6, 14);
724	  let sinceLast = 0;
725	  while (x < w - 6) {
726	    const sy = Math.floor(surface[x]);
727	    const relaxed = sinceLast >= 24;
728	    // 软装饰（草丛/花/蘑菇/树苗）不挡树：基座格判定忽略，种前清掉
729	    const isSoft = (t: number) => t === T.TALLGRASS || t === T.FLOWER || t === T.MUSHROOM || t === T.SAPLING;
730	    // 不种在水里：树干基座格（地面上一格）必须无液体
731	    if (store.get(x, sy) === T.GRASS && (!store.isActive(x, sy - 1) || isSoft(store.get(x, sy - 1))) && store.liquid[store.idx(x, sy - 1)] === 0) {
732	      const th = rng.int(7, 14);
733	      let ok = true;
734	      for (let dy = 1; dy <= th; dy++) {
735	        if (store.isActive(x, sy - dy) && !(dy === 1 && isSoft(store.get(x, sy - dy)))) { ok = false; break; }
736	      }
737	      // 树冠 80×80 外扩 2 格：要求 ±2 列上方净空、地面实心；放宽时只查本列±1
738	      const range = relaxed ? 1 : 2;
739	      for (let dx = -range; dx <= range; dx++) {
740	        if ((store.isActive(x + dx, sy - 1) && !isSoft(store.get(x + dx, sy - 1))) || !(store.get(x + dx, sy) === T.GRASS || store.isSolid(x + dx, sy))) {
741	          ok = false; break;
742	        }
743	      }
744	      if (ok) {
745	        if (isSoft(store.get(x, sy - 1))) store.setTile(x, sy - 1, 0); // 清基座装饰
746	        // 原版 Tiles_5 22px 网格帧：树顶标记 (22,198) + 树干身 (0,0)
747	        for (let dy = 1; dy <= th; dy++) {
748	          store.setTileSilent(x, sy - dy, T.TREE, dy === th ? 22 : 0, dy === th ? 198 : 0);
749	        }
750	        // 侧枝：原版标记帧（44=左枝 / 66=右枝，y=198），干高 2..h-3 段两侧各 40%，
751	        // 每棵上限 2-4 枝（Tree_Branches 贴图由渲染端按标记帧绘制）
752	        const maxBranches = rng.int(2, 4);
753	        let branchCount = 0;
754	        for (let dy = 2; dy <= th - 3 && branchCount < maxBranches; dy++) {
755	          for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
756	            if (branchCount >= maxBranches) break;
757	            if (rng.int(0, 99) >= 40) continue;
758	            const bx = x + side, by = sy - dy;
759	            const bt = store.get(bx, by);
760	            const soft = bt === T.TALLGRASS || bt === T.FLOWER || bt === T.MUSHROOM || bt === T.SAPLING;
761	            if ((!store.isActive(bx, by) || soft) && store.get(bx, by + 1) !== T.TREE) {
762	              store.setTileSilent(bx, by, T.TREE, side < 0 ? 44 : 66, 198);
763	              branchCount++;
764	            }
765	          }
766	        }
767	        world.registerTree(x, sy - th, th);
768	        x += rng.int(6, 14);
769	        sinceLast = 0;
770	        continue;
771	      }
772	    }
773	    x++;
774	    sinceLast++;
775	  }
776	}
777	
778	// ---------- 结构（地下小屋 + 宝箱） ----------
779	function placeChest(store: GenCtx['store'], x: number, y: number) {
780	  // 2×2 宝箱 object（锚点在左上）
781	  store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.CHEST, 0, 0);
782	  store.setTileSilent(x + 1, y, T.CHEST, 1, 0);
783	  store.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, T.CHEST, 0, 1);
784	  store.setTileSilent(x + 1, y + 1, T.CHEST, 1, 1);
785	}
786	
787	function structurePass({ store, surface, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
788	  const { w, h } = store;
789	  const hutCount = 4;
790	  for (let i = 0; i < hutCount; i++) {
791	    const hx = rng.int(20, w - 30);
792	    const hy = rng.int(Math.floor(h * 0.45), h - 40);
793	    buildHut(store, hx, hy, rng);
794	    // 小屋中心宝箱（坐在地板上：地板在 hy+6）
795	    placeChest(store, hx + 4, hy + 4);
796	    world.chests.push({ x: hx + 4, y: hy + 4, items: Array(10).fill(null) });
797	  }
798	  // 地表/浅层散落宝箱
799	  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
800	    const cx = rng.int(10, w - 12);
801	    // 放在洞穴空地的地上（需要 2×2 空间）
802	    for (let y = Math.floor(surface[cx]) + 8; y < h - 10; y++) {
803	      const clear =
804	        !store.isActive(cx, y) && !store.isActive(cx + 1, y) &&
805	        !store.isActive(cx, y - 1) && !store.isActive(cx + 1, y - 1) &&
806	        !store.isActive(cx, y + 1) && !store.isActive(cx + 1, y + 1);
807	      if (clear && store.isSolid(cx, y + 2) && store.isSolid(cx + 1, y + 2)) {
808	        placeChest(store, cx, y);
809	        world.chests.push({ x: cx, y, items: Array(10).fill(null) });
810	        break;
811	      }
812	    }
813	  }
814	}
815	
816	function buildHut(store: GenCtx['store'], hx: number, hy: number, rng: RNG) {
817	  const rw = 9, rh = 6;
818	  // 清空内部
819	  for (let x = hx; x < hx + rw; x++) {
820	    for (let y = hy; y < hy + rh; y++) {
821	      store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
822	      store.setWallSilent(x, y, W.WOOD);
823	    }
824	  }
825	  // 地板与墙
826	  for (let x = hx - 1; x <= hx + rw; x++) {
827	    store.setTileSilent(x, hy + rh, T.WOOD);
828	    store.setTileSilent(x, hy - 1, T.WOOD);
829	  }
830	  for (let y = hy; y <= hy + rh; y++) {
831	    store.setTileSilent(hx - 1, y, T.WOOD);
832	    store.setTileSilent(hx + rw, y, T.WOOD);
833	  }
834	  // 门洞（左侧地板上方 3 格）
835	  for (let y = hy + rh - 3; y < hy + rh; y++) store.setTileSilent(hx - 1, y, 0);
836	  placeDoorClosed(store, hx - 1, hy + rh - 3, 0);
837	  // 内部陈设
838	  store.setTileSilent(hx + 1, hy + rh - 1, T.WORKBENCH, 0, 0);
839	  store.setTileSilent(hx + 3, hy + rh - 1, T.TORCH, 0, 0);
840	  if (rng.chance(0.5)) store.setTileSilent(hx + 5, hy + rh - 1, T.FURNACE, 0, 0);
841	}
842	
843	// ---------- 收尾：出生点（选最平坦区域并整平） ----------
844	function finalize({ store, surface, world, cfg }: GenCtx) {
845	  // 幽灵净化:生成期 TileRunner 会在空气格写残留 type(原版语义,渲染不可见),
846	  // 但我们的存档格式无 active 位——进入存档前归零(防重读激活成浮空块)
847	  for (let i = 0; i < store.type.length; i++) {
848	    if (!store.flags[i] && store.type[i] !== 0) store.type[i] = 0; // 幽灵 type
849	    if (store.type[i] === 0) store.flags[i] = 0; // 幻影活性(type 0 不可有 flags)
850	  }
851	  const cx = Math.floor(store.w / 2);
852	  // 在中心 ±60 列内找 9 列窗口高度方差最小的位置（平坦出生点，#14）
853	  let bestX = cx, bestVar = Infinity;
854	  for (let d = -60; d <= 60; d += 3) {
855	    const x0 = cx + d - 4;
856	    if (x0 < 4 || x0 + 8 > store.w - 5) continue;
857	    const hs: number[] = [];
858	    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k++) hs.push(surface[x0 + k]);
859	    const avg = hs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 9;
860	    const v = hs.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - avg) ** 2, 0);
861	    if (v < bestVar) { bestVar = v; bestX = x0 + 4; }
862	  }
863	  const sx = bestX;
864	  const flatY = Math.round(surface.slice(sx - 4, sx + 5).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 9);
865	  // 整平：±5 列统一到 flatY（填/挖），草皮封顶，上方 5 格清空，清除区内树木（防浮空树）
866	  for (let dx = -5; dx <= 5; dx++) {
867	    const x = sx + dx;
868	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
869	      const syF = flatY;
870	      if (y < syF) {
871	        if (store.isActive(x, y)) store.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
872	        store.setLiquid(x, y, 0, 0);
873	      } else if (y === syF) {
874	        store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.GRASS);
875	        store.setLiquid(x, y, 0, 0);
876	      } else if (y > syF && y < flatY + 20 && !store.isActive(x, y)) {
877	        store.setTileSilent(x, y, T.DIRT);
878	      }
879	    }
880	    surface[x] = flatY;
881	  }
882	  const sy = flatY;
883	  world.spawnX = sx;
884	  world.spawnY = Math.max(4, sy - 3);
885	  // 原版管线的地表/岩石线来自 TerrainPass 输出,旧 fbm 常数仅作回退
886	  const gs = ctxGs[0];
887	  if (gs) {
888	    world.groundLevel = gs.worldSurface;
889	    world.rockLevel = gs.rockLevel;
890	  } else {
891	    world.groundLevel = Math.floor(store.h * 0.28);
892	    world.rockLevel = Math.floor(store.h * 0.33);
893	  }
894	  // 原版树样式：4 等分横向区，全部森林样式 0
895	  world.treeX = [Math.floor(store.w / 4), Math.floor(store.w / 2), Math.floor(store.w * 3 / 4)];
896	  world.treeStyle = [0, 0, 0, 0];
897	  // 出生点上空净空
898	  for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
899	    for (let dy = 1; dy <= 5; dy++) {
900	      if (store.isActive(sx + dx, sy - dy)) {
901	        const t = store.get(sx + dx, sy - dy);
902	        if (t !== T.GRASS) store.setTileSilent(sx + dx, sy - dy, 0);
903	      }
904	    }
905	  }
906	  // 清除整平区内登记的树（防幽灵树）
907	  world.trees = world.trees.filter((t) => Math.abs(t.x - sx) > 5);
908	}
909

1	// 阶段 4 结构 pass:
2	// - FloatingIslands(pass 19,L5552 + CloudIsland L47397 简化):云岛(云 189 壳 + 土芯 + 草皮)
3	// - LifeCrystals(pass 55,L7828):area×2e-5 个生命水晶(岩层空腔地上)
4	// - HellHouses(L16085 AddHellHouses 简化):地狱中段黑曜石砖屋
5	// - SurfaceDecor:生物群系感知地表(丛林/雪原树密度、沙漠仙人掌、丛林藤蔓/蘑菇)
6	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
7	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
8	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
9	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
10	import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/items';
11	import { digTunnel } from './TileRunner';
12	
13	const CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_189_cloud_block']!;
14	const EBONSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['v_112_ebonsand_block']!;
15	const CRIMSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['v_234_crimsand_block']!;
16	const JUNGLE_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!;
17	const CACTUS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_80_cactus']!;
18	const VINE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_52_vines']!;
19	const HEART = TILE_BY_KEY['crystal_heart']!;
20	const OBSIDIAN_BRICK = TILE_BY_KEY['v_75_obsidian_brick']!;
21	const HELL_BRICK = TILE_BY_KEY['v_76_obsidian_brick'] ?? OBSIDIAN_BRICK;
22	
23	/** 浮空岛:数量 w×0.0008,避开中部 ±150(缩放)与其他岛 ±180 */
24	export function runFloatingIslandsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
25	  const { w, h } = st;
26	  const van = w / 4200;
27	  const count = Math.max(1, Math.floor(w * 0.0008));
28	  const placed: number[] = [];
29	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
30	    let x = 0, ok = false;
31	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 200 && !ok; attempt++) {
32	      x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
33	      while (x > w / 2 - 150 * Math.max(0.4, van) && x < w / 2 + 150 * Math.max(0.4, van)) {
34	        x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
35	      }
36	      ok = placed.every((px) => Math.abs(px - x) >= 180 * Math.max(0.4, van));
37	    }
38	    // 该列地表
39	    let surf = 0;
40	    let found = false;
41	    for (let y = Math.floor(200 * Math.max(0.3, van)); y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
42	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { surf = y; found = true; break; }
43	    }
44	    if (!found) continue;
45	    const y = Math.min(rng.int(90, Math.max(91, surf - 100)), gs.worldSurfaceLow - 50);
46	    cloudIsland(st, rng, x, y);
47	    placed.push(x);
48	    gs.floatingIslands.push({ x, y, style: 0, isLake: false });
49	  }
50	}
51	
52	/** CloudIsland(L47397)七阶段移植(核心部分):扁平云盘(纵压3+顶面游走)→
53	 * 土芯只嵌云内 → 内部云墙 73 → 10% 水池。岛屋由独立 pass 处理(暂缺)。 */
54	function cloudIsland(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, j: number): void {
55	  const CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_189_cloud_block']!;
56	  // ---- 阶段1:云壳(随机走椭圆,纵压 3,顶面 ±1 游走)----
57	  let num1 = rng.int(100, 149);
58	  let steps = rng.int(20, 29);
59	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
60	  let vx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.2;
61	  while (vx > -2 && vx < 2) vx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.2;
62	  let vy = rng.int(-20, -11) * 0.02;
63	  let bboxX0 = i, bboxX1 = i, bboxY0 = j, bboxY1 = j;
64	  while (num1 > 0 && steps > 0) {
65	    num1 -= rng.int(0, 3);
66	    steps--;
67	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px - num1 * 0.5)), x1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor(px + num1 * 0.5));
68	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py - num1 * 0.5)), y1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.floor(py + num1 * 0.5));
69	    const r = num1 * rng.int(80, 119) * 0.01;
70	    let top = py + 1; // 每列顶面游走(钳 [py, py+2])
71	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
72	      if (rng.next() < 0.5) top += rng.int(-1, 1);
73	      top = Math.max(py, Math.min(py + 2, top));
74	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
75	        if (y <= top) continue;
76	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
77	        if (Math.hypot(x - px, (y - py) * 3) < r * 0.4) {
78	          const ti = st.idx(x, y);
79	          st.type[ti] = CLOUD; st.flags[ti] = 1;
80	          bboxX0 = Math.min(bboxX0, x); bboxX1 = Math.max(bboxX1, x);
81	          bboxY0 = Math.min(bboxY0, y); bboxY1 = Math.max(bboxY1, y);
82	        }
83	      }
84	    }
85	    px += vx; py += vy;
86	    vx += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.05;
87	    vx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vx));
88	    vy = Math.max(-0.2, Math.min(0.2, vy));
89	  }
90	  if (bboxX1 <= bboxX0) return;
91	  // ---- 阶段3:土芯(80-95,只把云换土)----
92	  let dR = rng.int(80, 94);
93	  let dSteps = rng.int(10, 14);
94	  let dx0 = i + 0.0, dy0 = bboxY0 + 0.0;
95	  let dvx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.2;
96	  while (dvx > -2 && dvx < 2) dvx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.2;
97	  let dvy = rng.int(-20, -11) * 0.02;
98	  while (dR > 0 && dSteps > 0) {
99	    dR -= rng.int(0, 3);
100	    dSteps--;
101	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(dx0 - dR * 0.5)), x1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor(dx0 + dR * 0.5));
102	    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(dy0 - dR * 0.5)), y1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.floor(dy0 + dR * 0.5));
103	    const r = dR * rng.int(80, 119) * 0.01;
104	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
105	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
106	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
107	        const ti = st.idx(x, y);
108	        if (st.type[ti] === CLOUD && Math.hypot(x - dx0, (y - dy0) * 3) < r * 0.4) {
109	          st.type[ti] = T.DIRT;
110	        }
111	      }
112	    }
113	    dx0 += dvx; dy0 += dvy;
114	    dvx += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.05;
115	    dvx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, dvx));
116	    dvy = Math.max(-0.2, Math.min(0.2, dvy));
117	  }
118	  // ---- 阶段5:内部云墙(3×3 全包裹 → wall 73)----
119	  for (let x = bboxX0; x <= bboxX1; x++) {
120	    for (let y = bboxY0; y <= bboxY1; y++) {
121	      let all = true;
122	      for (let ax = -1; ax <= 1 && all; ax++) {
123	        for (let ay = -1; ay <= 1; ay++) {
124	          if (!st.inBounds(x + ax, y + ay) || !st.flags[st.idx(x + ax, y + ay)]) { all = false; break; }
125	        }
126	      }
127	      if (all) st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] = 73;
128	    }
129	  }
130	  // ---- 阶段6:水池(每列 10% 在云顶凹处放水)----
131	  for (let x = bboxX0 + 2; x < bboxX1 - 2; x++) {
132	    if (rng.next() >= 0.1) continue;
133	    let y = Math.max(1, bboxY0 - 10);
134	    while (y < st.h - 1 && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) y++;
135	    const below = st.idx(x, y + 1);
136	    if (y < bboxY1 && st.type[below] === CLOUD) {
137	      const ti = st.idx(x, y);
138	      st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0;
139	      st.liquid[ti] = 255; st.liquidType[ti] = 1;
140	    }
141	  }
142	}
143	
144	/** 生命水晶(pass 55):area×2e-5,岩层空腔地面 */
145	export function runLifeCrystalsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
146	  const { w, h } = st;
147	  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface * 2 + gs.rockLevel) / 3);
148	  const count = Math.floor(w * h * 0.00002);
149	  let placed = 0;
150	  for (let n = 0; n < count * 30 && placed < count; n++) {
151	    const x = rng.int(40, w - 41);
152	    const y = rng.int(yMin, Math.max(yMin + 1, h - 300));
153	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y + 1) || !st.inBounds(x + 1, y + 1)) continue;
154	    // 2×2 净空 + 下方实心
155	    let clear = true;
156	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
157	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
158	        if (st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y - dy)]) { clear = false; break; }
159	      }
160	    }
161	    if (!clear || !st.isSolid(x, y + 1) || !st.isSolid(x + 1, y + 1)) continue;
162	    // 原版 AddLifeCrystal(cs:16023-16038):顶行 frameY=0、底行 18(此前写反致贴图上下颠倒)
163	    st.setTileSilent(x, y - 1, HEART, 0, 0);
164	    st.setTileSilent(x + 1, y - 1, HEART, 18, 0);
165	    st.setTileSilent(x, y, HEART, 0, 18);
166	    st.setTileSilent(x + 1, y, HEART, 18, 18);
167	    placed++;
168	  }
169	}
170	
171	/** 地狱屋(L16085 简化):地狱中段黑曜石砖小屋(空心 + 砖墙背景) */
172	export function runHellHousesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
173	  const { w, h } = st;
174	  const hellTop = h - 200; // 地狱层顶部(Underworld 边界 h-190..h-160)
175	  if (hellTop >= h - 60) return;
176	  const count = Math.max(2, Math.round(w / 4200 * 6));
177	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
178	    const hx = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.85) - 14);
179	    const hy = rng.int(hellTop + 20, h - 90);
180	    const rw = rng.int(10, 16), rh = rng.int(5, 7);
181	    // 空心 + 墙
182	    for (let x = hx; x < hx + rw; x++) {
183	      for (let y = hy; y < hy + rh; y++) {
184	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
185	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
186	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
187	        st.wall[i] = 14; // HellstoneBrickUnsafe
188	      }
189	    }
190	    // 砖壳
191	    for (let x = hx - 1; x <= hx + rw; x++) {
192	      for (let y = hy - 1; y <= hy + rh; y++) {
193	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
194	        const border = x === hx - 1 || x === hx + rw || y === hy - 1 || y === hy + rh;
195	        if (border) {
196	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
197	          st.type[i] = rng.next() < 0.8 ? OBSIDIAN_BRICK : HELL_BRICK;
198	          st.flags[i] = 1;
199	          st.liquid[i] = 0;
200	        }
201	      }
202	    }
203	    // 开口(左右各去 2 砖)
204	    for (let y = hy + rh - 3; y <= hy + rh; y++) {
205	      for (const dx of [hx - 1, hx + rw]) {
206	        if (st.inBounds(dx, y)) {
207	          const i = st.idx(dx, y);
208	          st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
209	        }
210	      }
211	    }
212	    // 灰烬基座(原版房屋坐落在地狱灰烬地表上,我们补一座墩)
213	    const ash = T.ASH;
214	    for (let x = hx - 1; x <= hx + rw; x++) {
215	      for (let y = hy + rh + 1; y < Math.min(st.h, hy + rh + 30); y++) {
216	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
217	        if (st.flags[i]) break;
218	        st.type[i] = ash; st.flags[i] = 1;
219	      }
220	    }
221	  }
222	}

224	/** 地表装饰:按生物群系种树/仙人掌/藤蔓/蘑菇(原版 pass 60+ 系列简化) */
225	export function runSurfaceDecorPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
226	  const { w, h } = st;
227	  const isSoft = (t: number) => t === T.TALLGRASS || t === T.FLOWER || t === T.MUSHROOM || t === VINE;
228	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
229	  const inDesert = (x: number) => !!ud && x >= ud.x0 && x <= ud.x1;
230	  let x = rng.int(6, 14);
231	  let sinceLast = 0;
232	  while (x < w - 6) {
233	    let sy = -1;
234	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
235	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { sy = y; break; }
236	    }
237	    if (sy >= 0) {
238	      const ground = st.type[st.idx(x, sy)];
239	      const relaxed = sinceLast >= 24;
240	      // ---- 树(草/丛林草/雪)----
241	      if ((ground === T.GRASS || ground === JUNGLE_GRASS || ground === T.SNOW)
242	        && (!st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - 1)] || isSoft(st.type[st.idx(x, sy - 1)]))) {
243	        const jungle = ground === JUNGLE_GRASS;
244	        const th = jungle ? rng.int(8, 16) : rng.int(7, 14);
245	        let ok = true;
246	        for (let dy = 1; dy <= th; dy++) {
247	          if (st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - dy)] && !(dy === 1 && isSoft(st.type[st.idx(x, sy - dy)]))) { ok = false; break; }
248	        }
249	        const range = relaxed ? 1 : 2;
250	        for (let dx = -range; dx <= range && ok; dx++) {
251	          if ((st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, sy - 1)] && !isSoft(st.type[st.idx(x + dx, sy - 1)]))
252	            || !st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, sy)]) { ok = false; break; }
253	        }
254	        if (ok) {
255	          if (isSoft(st.type[st.idx(x, sy - 1)])) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, 0);
256	          for (let dy = 1; dy <= th; dy++) {
257	            st.setTileSilent(x, sy - dy, T.TREE, dy === th ? 22 : 0, dy === th ? 198 : 0);
258	          }
259	          const maxBranches = rng.int(2, 4);
260	          let branchCount = 0;
261	          for (let dy = 2; dy <= th - 3 && branchCount < maxBranches; dy++) {
262	            for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
263	              if (branchCount >= maxBranches) break;
264	              if (rng.int(0, 99) >= 40) continue;
265	              const bx = x + side, by = sy - dy;
266	              if ((!st.flags[st.idx(bx, by)] || isSoft(st.type[st.idx(bx, by)])) && st.type[st.idx(bx, by + 1)] !== T.TREE) {
267	                st.setTileSilent(bx, by, T.TREE, side < 0 ? 44 : 66, 198);
268	                branchCount++;
269	              }
270	            }
271	          }
272	          x += jungle ? rng.int(5, 10) : rng.int(6, 14);
273	          sinceLast = 0;
274	          continue;
275	        }
276	      }
277	      // ---- 仙人掌(沙漠沙/邪恶沙面)----
278	      if ((ground === T.SAND || ground === EBONSAND || ground === CRIMSAND) && inDesert(x)
279	        && !st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - 1)] && rng.next() < 0.12) {
280	        const ch = rng.int(2, 5);
281	        let ok = true;
282	        for (let dy = 1; dy <= ch; dy++) if (st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - dy)]) { ok = false; break; }
283	        if (ok) {
284	          for (let dy = 1; dy <= ch; dy++) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - dy, CACTUS, 0, 0);
285	          x += rng.int(4, 9);
286	          sinceLast = 0;
287	          continue;
288	        }
289	      }
290	      // ---- 杂草/花/蘑菇 ----(藤蔓在下方统一扫描)
291	      // ---- 杂草/花/蘑菇 ----
292	      if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, sy - 1)] && (ground === T.GRASS || ground === JUNGLE_GRASS || ground === T.SNOW)) {
293	        const r = rng.next();
294	        if (r < 0.3) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.TALLGRASS, [0, 18, 36, 54, 72, 90][rng.int(0, 5)], 0);
295	        else if (r < 0.42) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.FLOWER, [108, 126, 162, 180, 216, 252, 342, 540, 594][rng.int(0, 8)], 0);
296	        else if (r < 0.45 && ground !== T.SNOW) st.setTileSilent(x, sy - 1, T.MUSHROOM, 144, 0);
297	      }
298	    }
299	    x++;
300	    sinceLast++;
301	  }
302	  // ---- 藤蔓:挂在悬空草根/草皮下方(洞穴顶/崖底)----
303	  for (let vx = 2; vx < w - 2; vx++) {
304	    for (let vy = 2; vy < h - 2; vy++) {
305	      const i = st.idx(vx, vy);
306	      if (st.type[i] !== JUNGLE_GRASS && st.type[i] !== T.GRASS) continue;
307	      if (!st.flags[i] || st.flags[st.idx(vx, vy + 1)]) continue;
308	      if (st.liquid[st.idx(vx, vy + 1)] > 0) continue;
309	      if (rng.next() < 0.5) continue;
310	      const len = rng.int(1, 8);
311	      for (let k = 1; k <= len; k++) {
312	        const ii = st.idx(vx, vy + k);
313	        if (vy + k >= h - 1 || st.flags[ii] || st.type[ii] !== 0) break;
314	        st.setTileSilent(vx, vy + k, VINE, 0, 0);
315	      }
316	      vy += 10; // 稀疏
317	    }
318	  }
319	}
320	
321	const SANDSTONE_BRICK = TILE_BY_KEY['v_151_sandstone_brick']!;
322	const CHEST = TILE_BY_KEY['chest']!;
323	
324	/** 金字塔(pass 38 Pyramids 简化):gs.pyramidSpots 候选 → 地下沙岩砖金字塔 + 宝箱 */
325	export function runPyramidPass(
326	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
327	  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>,
328	): void {
329	  for (const spot of gs.pyramidSpots.slice(0, 2)) {
330	    const size = rng.int(14, 20); // 半宽
331	    const baseY = Math.min(st.h - 260, spot.y + rng.int(60, 90));
332	    if (spot.x < size + 5 || spot.x > st.w - size - 5) continue;
333	    // 金字塔主体(逐层收窄)
334	    for (let layer = 0; layer < size; layer++) {
335	      const halfW = size - layer;
336	      for (let x = spot.x - halfW; x <= spot.x + halfW; x++) {
337	        for (let y = baseY - layer; y <= baseY - layer; y++) {
338	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
339	          st.type[i] = SANDSTONE_BRICK; st.flags[i] = 1;
340	          st.wall[i] = 34; // SandstoneBrickUnsafe
341	        }
342	      }
343	    }
344	    // 内腔(倒阶梯掏空)
345	    for (let layer = 2; layer < size - 2; layer++) {
346	      const halfW = size - layer - 2;
347	      for (let x = spot.x - halfW; x <= spot.x + halfW; x++) {
348	        const i = st.idx(x, baseY - layer);
349	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
350	      }
351	    }
352	    // 宝箱(内腔底部,cs L12417-12426):style1 金箱,战利品三选一
353	    // (857 沙暴瓶/848 法老面具/934 飞毯)
354	    const bx = spot.x, by = baseY - 2;
355	    st.setTileSilent(bx, by, CHEST, 1 * 36, 0);
356	    st.setTileSilent(bx + 1, by, CHEST, 1 * 36 + 18, 0);
357	    st.setTileSilent(bx, by + 1, CHEST, 1 * 36, 18);
358	    st.setTileSilent(bx + 1, by + 1, CHEST, 1 * 36 + 18, 18);
359	    const lootKeys = ['vi_857_sandstorm_in_a_bottle', 'vi_848_PharaohsMask', 'vi_934_FlyingCarpet'];
360	    const lootId = ITEM_BY_KEY[lootKeys[rng.int(0, 2)]];
361	    chests.push({
362	      x: bx, y: by,
363	      items: lootId !== undefined ? [{ id: lootId, stack: 1 }, ...Array(9).fill(null)] : Array(10).fill(null),
364	    });
365	  }
366	}
367	
368	/** Wet Jungle(pass 43 简化):从丛林草 tile 起挖湿隧道(灌水) */
369	export function runWetJunglePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
370	  const core = (gs as any).jungleCoreX ?? gs.jungleX;
371	  const JG = JUNGLE_GRASS;
372	  const tunnels = Math.max(4, Math.round(st.w / 500));
373	  let dug = 0;
374	  for (let n = 0; n < tunnels * 60 && dug < tunnels; n++) {
375	    const x = core + rng.int(-Math.floor(st.w * 0.12), Math.floor(st.w * 0.12));
376	    const y = rng.int(gs.rockLevel, Math.max(gs.rockLevel + 1, st.h - 500));
377	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== JG || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) continue;
378	    const dx = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01, dy = rng.int(-100, 100) * 0.01;
379	    digTunnel(st, rng, x, y, dx, dy, rng.int(30, 120), rng.int(3, 6), true);
380	    dug++;
381	  }
382	}

1	// 原版 JunglePass 移植(GameContent/Biomes/JunglePass.cs,1:1)。
2	// 起点 (jungleOriginX, (h+rockLayer)/2):三轮随机移动 + 泥 runner(带宝石 63/65/67),
3	// 巨型泥 runner(num2=400-600×scale,steps 10000,向上 -20)+ 地表隧道,
4	// 泥墙挖洞(简化)+ 收尾(泥簇/石囊/宝石)。mudWall 期间铺丛林天然墙 64。
5	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
6	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
9	import { tileRunner } from './TileRunner';
10	
11	const MUD = TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;
12	const GEMS = ['gem_sapphire', 'gem_ruby', 'gem_emerald', 'gem_topaz', 'gem_amethyst', 'gem_diamond'].map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]!);
13	
14	export function runJunglePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): number {
15	  const { w, h } = st;
16	  const scale = (w / 4200) * 1.5;
17	  const van = w / 4200;
18	  let x = gs.jungleX;
19	  let y = Math.floor((h + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
20	  let zeroX = 0, zeroY = 0;
21	
22	  const applyRandomMovement = (xRange: number, yRange: number) => {
23	    x += rng.int(Math.floor(-xRange * scale), Math.floor(xRange * scale));
24	    y += rng.int(Math.floor(-yRange * scale), Math.floor(yRange * scale));
25	    y = Math.max(gs.rockLevel, Math.min(h - 1, y));
26	  };
27	  const placeFirstPassMud = (xSpeedScale: number) => {
28	    const str = rng.int(Math.floor(250 * scale), Math.floor(500 * scale));
29	    // 原版步数固定 50-150 不缩放(speedX=±3 → 漂移 ≤450;4200 宽的 10.7%)
30	    const stp = rng.int(50, 149);
31	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD, speedX: gs.dungeonSide * xSpeedScale, speedY: 0, mudWall: true });
32	  };
33	  const placeGemsAt = (baseGem: number, variants: number) => {
34	    for (let n = 0; n < 6 * scale; n++) {
35	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
36	        x + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale)),
37	        y + rng.int(-Math.floor(125 * scale), Math.floor(125 * scale)), {
38	          strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(3, 7),
39	          type: GEMS[baseGem + rng.int(0, variants - 1)],
40	        });
41	    }
42	  };
43	
44	  applyRandomMovement(100, 100);
45	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
46	  placeFirstPassMud(3);
47	  placeGemsAt(0, 2); // 蓝宝石 63
48	  applyRandomMovement(250, 150);
49	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
50	  placeFirstPassMud(0);
51	  placeGemsAt(2, 2); // 祖母绿 65
52	  const oldX = x, oldY = y;
53	  applyRandomMovement(400, 150);
54	  zeroX += x; zeroY += y;
55	  placeFirstPassMud(-3);
56	  placeGemsAt(4, 2); // 黄玉 67
57	
58	  // 巨型泥 runner:三次移动点的均值为中心
59	  const cx = Math.floor(zeroX / 3), cy = Math.floor(zeroY / 3);
60	  const num2 = rng.int(Math.floor(400 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
61	  const num3 = Math.floor(25 * scale);
62	  const i = Math.max(gs.beachLeftEnd + Math.floor(num2 / 2) + num3, Math.min(gs.beachRightStart - Math.floor(num2 / 2) - num3, cx));
63	  tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i, cy, { strength: num2, steps: 10000, type: MUD, speedY: -20, mudWall: true });
64	  // 泥柱中心记录给后续(丛林表面草化)
65	  (gs as any).jungleCoreX = i;
66	  const jungleXOut = generateTunnelToSurface(st, rng, gs, i, cy);
67	  generateHolesInMudWalls(st, rng, gs);
68	  generateFinishingTouches(st, rng, gs, oldX, oldY, scale, van);
69	  return jungleXOut;
70	}
71	
72	function generateTunnelToSurface(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, j: number): number {
73	  let radius = rng.int(5, 10);
74	  let px = i + 0.0, py = j + 0.0;
75	  let vx = rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
76	  let vy = rng.int(10, 19) * 0.1;
77	  let count = 0;
78	  let running = true;
79	  let jungleX = i;
80	  while (running) {
81	    if (py < gs.worldSurface) {
82	      const cx = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px)));
83	      const cy = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py)));
84	      // 隧道顶到达无墙无块的开放区即停(检查上方 1-5 格)
85	      let open = true;
86	      for (let d = 0; d <= 5; d++) {
87	        const ii = st.idx(cx, Math.max(5, cy - d));
88	        if (st.wall[ii] !== 0 || st.flags[ii]) { open = false; break; }
89	      }
90	      if (open) running = false;
91	    }
92	    jungleX = Math.floor(px);
93	    radius += rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.1;
94	    if (radius < 5) radius = 5;
95	    if (radius > 10) radius = 10;
96	    const x0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px - radius * 0.5)));
97	    const x1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.w - 10, Math.floor(px + radius * 0.5)));
98	    const y0 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py - radius * 0.5)));
99	    const y1 = Math.max(10, Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(py + radius * 0.5)));
100	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
101	      for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) {
102	        if (Math.abs(x - px) + Math.abs(y - py) < radius * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) {
103	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
104	        }
105	      }
106	    }
107	    count++;
108	    // 偶发分支洞穴
109	    if (count > 10 && rng.int(0, 49) < count) {
110	      count = 0;
111	      const side = rng.next() < 0.5 ? -2 : 2;
112	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, Math.floor(px), Math.floor(py), {
113	        strength: rng.int(3, 19), steps: rng.int(10, 99), type: -1, speedX: side,
114	      });
115	    }
116	    px += vx; py += vy;
117	    vy += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.01;
118	    if (vy > 0) vy = 0;
119	    if (vy < -2) vy = -2;
120	    vx += rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.1;
121	    if (px < i - 200) vx += rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
122	    if (px > i + 200) vx -= rng.int(5, 20) * 0.1;
123	    if (vx > 1.5) vx = 1.5;
124	    if (vx < -1.5) vx = -1.5;
125	    if (py < -50 || py > st.h + 50) running = false; // 安全阀
126	  }
127	  return jungleX;
128	}
129	
130	/** 泥墙挖洞(原版 MudWallRunner 的简化:在丛林墙上随机游走清出通道) */
131	function generateHolesInMudWalls(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
132	  const tries = Math.floor(st.w / 4);
133	  for (let n = 0; n < tries; n++) {
134	    let x = rng.int(20, st.w - 21);
135	    let y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, st.h - 150);
136	    let found = false;
137	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 40; attempt++) {
138	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
139	      if (st.wall[i] === 64 || st.wall[i] === 15) { found = true; break; }
140	      x = rng.int(20, st.w - 21);
141	      y = rng.int(gs.worldSurface + 10, st.h - 150);
142	    }
143	    if (!found) continue;
144	    // 简化随机走:清墙 30-80 步
145	    let steps = rng.int(30, 80);
146	    while (steps-- > 0) {
147	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) break;
148	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
149	      if (st.wall[i] === 64 || st.wall[i] === 15) st.wall[i] = 0;
150	      x += rng.int(-1, 1);
151	      y += rng.int(-1, 1);
152	    }
153	  }
154	}
155	
156	function generateFinishingTouches(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, oldX: number, oldY: number, scale: number, van: number): void {
157	  let x = oldX, y = oldY;
158	  // 大泥簇
159	  for (let n = 0; n <= 20 * scale; n++) {
160	    x += rng.int(Math.floor(-5 * scale), Math.floor(5 * scale));
161	    y += rng.int(Math.floor(-5 * scale), Math.floor(5 * scale));
162	    // 原版 300-500 步固定不缩放
163	    const str = rng.int(40, 99), stp = rng.int(300, 499);
164	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, x, y, { strength: str, steps: stp, type: MUD });
165	  }
166	  // 洞穴(1/7 填液体)
167	  for (let n = 0; n <= 10 * scale; n++) {
168	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
169	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
170	    let found = false;
171	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 40; attempt++) {
172	      if (i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD) { found = true; break; }
173	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
174	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
175	    }
176	    if (!found) continue;
177	    for (let k = 0; k < 8 * scale; k++) {
178	      i2 += rng.int(-30, 30);
179	      j2 += rng.int(-30, 30);
180	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i2, j2, {
181	        strength: rng.int(10, 19), steps: rng.int(30, 69),
182	        type: rng.next() * 7 < 1 ? -2 : -1,
183	      });
184	    }
185	  }
186	  // 泥内石囊 + 宝石
187	  for (let n = 0; n <= 300 * scale; n++) {
188	    let i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
189	    let j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
190	    let found = false;
191	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 40; attempt++) {
192	      if (i2 >= 1 && i2 < st.w - 1 && j2 >= 1 && j2 < st.h - 1 && st.type[st.idx(i2, j2)] === MUD) { found = true; break; }
193	      i2 = oldX + rng.int(-Math.floor(600 * scale), Math.floor(600 * scale));
194	      j2 = oldY + rng.int(-Math.floor(200 * scale), Math.floor(200 * scale));
195	    }
196	    if (!found) continue;
197	    tileRunner(st, rng, gs, i2, j2, { strength: rng.int(4, 9), steps: rng.int(5, 29), type: T.STONE });
198	    if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) {
199	      tileRunner(st, rng, gs,
200	        i2 + rng.int(-1, 1), j2 + rng.int(-1, 1),
201	        { strength: rng.int(3, 6), steps: rng.int(4, 7), type: GEMS[rng.int(0, 5)] });
202	    }
203	  }
204	}
205

1	// 原版 pass 3 "Ocean Sand"(L4962) + pass 33 "Beaches"(L6639) 移植。
2	// Ocean Sand:海滩区地表按深度随机走铺沙(50-200),顺手记录金字塔候选;
3	// Beaches:从海滩边缘挖出斜坡海盆( TuneOceanDepth 深度曲线),灌水,清墙,盆底铺沙。
4	// 原版固定 tile 常量按 4200 宽设计,此处按 w/4200 线性缩放保持比例(大世界 = 精确原版)。
5	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
6	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T } from '../../../data/tiles';
