10	/** Ocean Sand:海滩铺沙 + 金字塔候选(L4962-5042) */
11	export function runOceanSandPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
12	  const w = st.w;
13	  const sandDepthLimit = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
14	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
15	    // 拒绝采样:中部 40%-60% 区间的起点重掷(原版 while 循环)
16	    let x0 = rng.int(0, w - 1);
17	    while (x0 > w * 0.40 && x0 < w * 0.60) x0 = rng.int(0, w - 1);
18	    let left = rng.int(35, 89);
19	    if (i === 1) left += Math.floor(rng.int(20, 39) * (w / 4200));
20	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) left *= 2;
21	    if (i === 1) left *= 2;
22	    let right = rng.int(35, 89);
23	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) right *= 2;
24	    if (i === 1) right *= 2;
25	    let x1 = x0 - left, x2 = x0 + right;
26	    if (x1 < 0) x1 = 0;
27	    if (x2 > w) x2 = w;
28	    if (i === 1) continue; // 中段跳过(原版 case 1: continue)
29	    if (i === 0) { x1 = 0; x2 = gs.beachLeftEnd; }
30	    if (i === 2) { x1 = gs.beachRightStart; x2 = w; }
31	    // 沙层厚度随机走(50-200)
32	    let depth = rng.int(50, 99);
33	    for (let x = x1; x < x2; x++) {
34	      if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
35	        depth += rng.int(-1, 1);
36	        if (depth < 50) depth = 50;
37	        if (depth > 200) depth = 200;
38	      }
39	      for (let y = 0; y < sandDepthLimit; y++) {
40	        const ii = st.idx(x, y);
41	        if (!st.flags[ii]) continue;
42	        // 金字塔候选:区域中心 1/6 概率
43	        if (x === ((x1 + x2) >> 1) && rng.next() < 1 / 6) {
44	          gs.pyramidSpots.push({ x, y });
45	        }
46	        const edge = Math.min(depth, x - x1, x2 - x);
47	        const thick = edge + rng.int(0, 4);
48	        for (let y2 = y; y2 < y + thick && y2 < st.h; y2++) {
49	          const jj = st.idx(x, y2);
50	          if (x > x1 + rng.int(0, 4) && x < x2 - rng.int(0, 4)) {
51	            st.type[jj] = T.SAND;
52	          }
53	        }
54	        break;
55	      }
56	    }
57	  }
58	}
59	
60	/** TuneOceanDepth(L11682):逐列深度增量曲线,阈值按世界宽度比例缩放 */
61	function tuneOceanDepth(rng: RNG, count: number, depth: number, van: number, floridaStyle: boolean): number {
62	  const inc = (f: number) => depth + rng.int(10, 19) * f;
63	  const t = (n: number) => Math.max(1, Math.floor(n * van));
64	  if (!floridaStyle) {
65	    if (count < t(3)) return inc(0.2);
66	    if (count < t(6)) return inc(0.15);
67	    if (count < t(9)) return inc(0.1);
68	    if (count < t(15)) return inc(0.07);
69	    if (count < t(50)) return inc(0.05);
70	    if (count < t(75)) return inc(0.04);
71	    if (count < t(100)) return inc(0.03);
72	    if (count < t(125)) return inc(0.02);
73	    if (count < t(150)) return inc(0.01);
74	    if (count < t(175)) return inc(0.005);
75	    if (count < t(200)) return inc(0.001);
76	    if (count < t(230)) return inc(0.01);
77	    if (count < t(235)) return inc(0.05);
78	    if (count < t(240)) return inc(0.1);
79	    if (count < t(245)) return inc(0.05);
80	    if (count < t(255)) return inc(0.01);
81	    return depth;
82	  }
83	  // florida 变体:同阈值曲线,近岸增量极小、远岸陡增(L11719-11748)
84	  if (count < t(3)) return inc(0.001);
85	  if (count < t(6)) return inc(0.002);
86	  if (count < t(9)) return inc(0.004);
87	  if (count < t(15)) return inc(0.007);
88	  if (count < t(50)) return inc(0.01);
89	  if (count < t(75)) return inc(0.014);
90	  if (count < t(100)) return inc(0.019);
91	  if (count < t(125)) return inc(0.027);
92	  if (count < t(150)) return inc(0.038);
93	  if (count < t(175)) return inc(0.052);
94	  if (count < t(200)) return inc(0.08);
95	  if (count < t(230)) return inc(0.12);
96	  if (count < t(235)) return inc(0.16);
97	  if (count < t(240)) return inc(0.27);
98	  if (count < t(245)) return inc(0.43);
99	  if (count < t(255)) return inc(0.6);
100	  return depth;
101	}
102	
103	/** Beaches:挖海盆灌水(L6639-6728) */
104	export function runBeachesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
105	  const w = st.w;
106	  const van = w / 4200;
107	  const sc = (n: number) => Math.max(1, Math.round(n * van));
108	  const oceanWaterMin = sc(220), oceanWaterMax = sc(260), forcedJungleLen = sc(275), minSand = sc(50);
109	  const edgeWall = Math.max(4, Math.round(30 * van));
110	
111	  let floridaL = false, floridaR = false;
112	  if (rng.next() < 0.25) {
113	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) floridaL = true; else floridaR = true;
114	  }
115	
116	  for (let side = 0; side < 2; side++) {
117	    if (side === 0) {
118	      // 左海盆:从 beachLeftEnd-50 之左往世界缘挖
119	      let waterX = rng.int(oceanWaterMin, oceanWaterMax - 1);  // 原版 Next(220,260) 上界开
120	      if (gs.dungeonSide === 1) waterX = forcedJungleLen; // 丛林侧强制 275(缩放)
121	      const cap = gs.beachLeftEnd - minSand;
122	      if (waterX > cap) waterX = Math.max(2, cap);
123	      // 该列地表
124	      let surfY = 0;
125	      while (!st.flags[st.idx(waterX - 1, surfY)]) surfY++;
126	      const waterY = surfY + rng.int(1, 4);
127	      let count = 0, depth = 1;
128	      for (let x = waterX - 1; x >= 0; x--) {
129	        if (x > edgeWall) { count++; depth = tuneOceanDepth(rng, count, depth, van, floridaL); }
130	        else depth += 1;
131	        const pad = rng.int(15, 19);
132	        const yEnd = waterY + depth + pad;
133	        for (let y = 0; y < yEnd && y < st.h; y++) {
134	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
135	          if (y < waterY + depth * 0.75 - 3) {
136	            st.flags[i] = 0; st.type[i] = 0; st.frameX[i] = 0; st.frameY[i] = 0;
137	            if (y > waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
138	            else if (y === waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 127; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
139	          } else if (y > waterY) {
140	            st.type[i] = T.SAND; st.flags[i] = 1;
141	          }
142	          st.wall[i] = 0;
143	        }
144	      }
145	    } else {
146	      // 右海盆:镜像
147	      let waterX = w - rng.int(oceanWaterMin, oceanWaterMax - 1);  // 上界开,同原版 Next
148	      if (gs.dungeonSide === -1) waterX = w - forcedJungleLen;
149	      const cap = gs.beachRightStart + minSand;
150	      if (waterX < cap) waterX = Math.min(w - 2, cap);
151	      let surfY = 0;
152	      while (!st.flags[st.idx(waterX, surfY)]) surfY++;
153	      const waterY = surfY + rng.int(1, 4);
154	      let count = 0, depth = 1;
155	      for (let x = waterX; x < w; x++) {
156	        if (x < w - edgeWall) { count++; depth = tuneOceanDepth(rng, count, depth, van, floridaR); }
157	        else depth += 1;
158	        const pad = rng.int(15, 19);
159	        const yEnd = waterY + depth + pad;
160	        for (let y = 0; y < yEnd && y < st.h; y++) {
161	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
162	          if (y < waterY + depth * 0.75 - 3) {
163	            st.flags[i] = 0; st.type[i] = 0; st.frameX[i] = 0; st.frameY[i] = 0;
164	            if (y > waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 255; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
165	            else if (y === waterY) { st.liquid[i] = 127; st.liquidType[i] = 1; }
166	          } else if (y > waterY) {
167	            st.type[i] = T.SAND; st.flags[i] = 1;
168	          }
169	          st.wall[i] = 0;
170	        }
171	      }
172	    }
173	  }
174	}
175

7	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
8	import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
9	
10	export function runIceBiomePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
11	  const { w, h } = st;
12	  gs.snowTop = gs.worldSurface;
13	  const num2 = gs.lavaLine - rng.int(160, 199); // 完整转换深度线
14	  let left = gs.snowOriginLeft, right = gs.snowOriginRight;
15	  let thickness = 10;
16	  gs.snowMinX = new Float32Array(h);
17	  gs.snowMaxX = new Float32Array(h);
18	
19	  const CORRUPT_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_23_corrupt_grass_block']!;
20	  const CLAY = TILE_BY_KEY['clay']!;
21	  const ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['ice']!;
22	
23	  for (let y = 0; y <= gs.lavaLine - 140; y++) {
24	    left += rng.int(-4, 3);
25	    right += rng.int(-3, 4);
26	    if (y > 0) {
27	      left = (left + gs.snowMinX[y - 1]) / 2;
28	      right = (right + gs.snowMaxX[y - 1]) / 2;
29	    }
30	    // 雪原整体向地牢侧缓慢漂移
31	    if (gs.dungeonSide > 0) {
32	      if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) { left++; right++; }
33	    } else if (rng.next() * 4 < 1) { left--; right--; }
34	    gs.snowMinX[y] = left;
35	    gs.snowMaxX[y] = right;
36	    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(left)), x1 = Math.min(w, Math.ceil(right));
37	    for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
38	      if (y < num2) {
39	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
40	        // 原版只写 type 不激活——未激活格保持空气,跳过
41	        if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
42	        if (st.wall[i] === 2) st.wall[i] = 40;
43	        const t = st.type[i];
44	        if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS || t === CORRUPT_GRASS || t === CLAY || t === T.SAND) st.type[i] = T.SNOW;
45	        else if (t === T.STONE) st.type[i] = ICE;
46	      } else {
47	        thickness += rng.int(-3, 3);
48	        if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) {
49	          thickness += rng.int(-4, 4);
50	          if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) thickness += rng.int(-6, 6);
51	        }
52	        if (thickness < 0) thickness = rng.int(0, 2);
53	        else if (thickness > 50) thickness = 50 - rng.int(0, 2);
54	        for (let y2 = y; y2 < y + thickness && y2 < h; y2++) {
55	          const i = st.idx(x, y2);
56	          if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
57	          if (st.wall[i] === 2) st.wall[i] = 40;
58	          const t = st.type[i];
59	          if (t === T.DIRT || t === T.GRASS || t === CORRUPT_GRASS || t === CLAY || t === T.SAND) st.type[i] = T.SNOW;
60	          else if (t === T.STONE) st.type[i] = ICE;
61	        }
62	      }
63	    }
64	    if (gs.snowBottom < y) gs.snowBottom = y;
65	  }
66	}

6	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
7	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
8	import type { GenState } from './GenState';

11	const MUD = TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;

13	const SNOW = TILE_BY_KEY['snow']!;
14	const ICE = TILE_BY_KEY['ice']!;
15	const SAND = T.SAND;
16	const SANDSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['sandstone']!;
17	const HARDSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand']!;
18	const FOSSIL = TILE_BY_KEY['desert_fossil']!;
19	
20	export function runDesertPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
21	  const { w, h } = st;
22	  const factor = w / 4200;
23	  // 沙漠 X(地牢异侧):x = w/2 + (rand(w/2)/8 + w/16) * -dungeonSide
24	  const half = Math.floor(w / 2);
25	  let num2 = 0, side = gs.dungeonSide, tries = 0;
26	  let x = 0;
27	  for (;;) {
28	    const off = Math.floor(rng.next() * half / 8) + Math.floor(half / 8);
29	    x = half + off * -side;
30	    if (++tries > Math.floor(w / 4)) { side = -side as 1 | -1; tries = 0; if (++num2 >= 2) break; }
31	    if (rowIsValid(st, x, factor)) break;
32	    if (tries > Math.floor(w / 2)) break;
33	  }
34	
35	  // DesertDescription.CreateFromPlacement
36	  const blockCols = Math.max(16, Math.floor(80 * factor));
37	  const blockRows = Math.max(40, Math.floor((rng.next() + 1) * 170 * factor));
38	  const width = Math.min(w - 20, Math.floor(4 * blockCols));
39	  const ox = Math.max(10, Math.min(w - 10 - width, x - Math.floor(width / 2)));
40	  // SurfaceMap:平均/最底(钳到 worldSurface-10)
41	  let surfSum = 0, surfMax = 0, count = 0;
42	  for (let i = ox; i < ox + width; i += 4) {
43	    let y = 0;
44	    while (y < h - 2 && !st.flags[st.idx(i, y)]) y++;
45	    surfSum += y; surfMax = Math.max(surfMax, y); count++;
46	  }
47	  const avg = surfSum / Math.max(1, count);
48	  const topY = Math.floor((avg + surfMax) / 2);
49	  const hiveTop = topY + rng.int(40, 59);
50	  const hiveHeight = Math.floor(2 * blockRows);
51	  const hiveBottom = Math.min(h - 2, hiveTop + hiveHeight);
52	  const desertBottom = hiveTop + Math.floor((hiveBottom - hiveTop) / 2);
53	
54	  // ---- SandMound.Place 1:1 ----
55	  const moundH = Math.floor((hiveBottom - hiveTop) / 2);
56	  let coarse = 0, fine = 0; // ±10 双随机游走
57	  for (let dx = -5; dx < width + 5; dx++) {
58	    const cx = ox + dx;
59	    if (cx < 0 || cx >= w) continue;
60	    const t = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, Math.abs((dx + 5) / (width + 10)) * 2 - 1));
61	    if (dx % 3 === 0) coarse = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, coarse + rng.int(-1, 1)));
62	    fine = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, fine + rng.int(-1, 1)));
63	    const profile = Math.sqrt(1 - Math.pow(t, 4));
64	    const moundSurfY = (topY + moundH) - Math.floor(profile * moundH) + coarse;
65	    // 中心削平/边缘抬升(SmoothStep(0.5,0.8,|t|)³)
66	    if (Math.abs(t) < 1) {
67	      const s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (Math.abs(t) - 0.5) / 0.3));
68	      const ss = s * s * (3 - 2 * s);
69	      const flatten = Math.min(10 + Math.floor((topY - Math.pow(ss, 3) * 20)) + fine, moundSurfY);
70	      // 从地表上一格清到 flatten(削掉沙丘上方突出物)
71	      let sy = 0;
72	      while (sy < h - 2 && !st.flags[st.idx(cx, sy)]) sy++;
73	      for (let y = sy - 1; y < flatten && y >= 0; y++) {
74	        const i = st.idx(cx, y);
75	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 0;
76	      }
77	    }
78	    // 纯沙柱:从 moundSurfY 填到 hiveBottom(整柱)
79	    for (let y = moundSurfY; y < hiveBottom; y++) {
80	      if (y < 0 || y >= h) continue;
81	      const i = st.idx(cx, y);
82	      st.type[i] = SAND; st.flags[i] = 1;
83	      st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
84	    }
85	  }
86	
87	  // ---- DesertHive(DesertHive.cs 1:1):椭圆散点 → 深度2 DFS 簇 → AttemptClaim 合并
88	  // 场强 = 各簇内全部块的 Σ1/d²,取前二大簇求和;阈值 3.5/1.8/0.7/0.25 ----
89	  const cellW = 4, cellH = 2;
90	  const gw = blockCols, gh = blockRows;
91	  // 1) 椭圆掩码 50% 散点(ClusterGroup.Generate)
92	  const blockMap: boolean[][] = [];
93	  for (let c = 0; c < gw; c++) blockMap.push(new Array(gh).fill(false));
94	  {
95	    const num1 = Math.floor(gw / 2) - 1, y1 = Math.max(1, Math.floor(gh / 2) - 1);
96	    const num2 = (num1 + 1) * (num1 + 1);
97	    for (let r = 0; r < gh; r++) {
98	      const num3 = (num1 / y1) * (r - y1);
99	      const num4 = Math.min(num1, Math.floor(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, num2 - num3 * num3))));
100	      for (let c = num1 - num4; c <= num1 + num4; c++) {
101	        if (c < 0 || c >= gw) continue;
102	        blockMap[c][r] = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;
103	      }
104	    }
105	  }
106	  // 2) 深度 2 DFS 搜簇(起点再过 50%),>2 点保留
107	  const pointClusters: Array<Array<[number, number]>> = [];
108	  {
109	    const dfs = (x: number, y: number, level: number, out: Array<[number, number]>) => {
110	      out.push([x, y]);
111	      blockMap[x][y] = false;
112	      level--;
113	      if (level === -1) return;
114	      if (x > 0 && blockMap[x - 1][y]) dfs(x - 1, y, level, out);
115	      if (x < gw - 1 && blockMap[x + 1][y]) dfs(x + 1, y, level, out);
116	      if (y > 0 && blockMap[x][y - 1]) dfs(x, y - 1, level, out);
117	      if (y < gh - 1 && blockMap[x][y + 1]) dfs(x, y + 1, level, out);
118	    };
119	    for (let x = 0; x < gw; x++) {
120	      for (let y = 0; y < gh; y++) {
121	        if (blockMap[x][y] && rng.int(0, 1) === 0) {
122	          const pc: Array<[number, number]> = [];
123	          dfs(x, y, 2, pc);
124	          if (pc.length > 2) pointClusters.push(pc);
125	        }
126	      }
127	    }
128	  }
129	  // 3) 索引图 + AttemptClaim:邻簇随机整体并入或删除
130	  const idxMap: number[][] = [];
131	  for (let c = 0; c < gw; c++) idxMap.push(new Array(gh).fill(-1));
132	  pointClusters.forEach((pc, i) => { for (const [px, py] of pc) idxMap[px][py] = i; });
133	  for (let i = 0; i < pointClusters.length; i++) {
134	    for (const [px, py] of pointClusters[i]) {
135	      if (idxMap[px][py] === -1) break;
136	      const ci = idxMap[px][py];
137	      const claim = (nx: number, ny: number) => {
138	        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= gw || ny >= gh) return;
139	        const other = idxMap[nx][ny];
140	        if (other === -1 || other === ci) return;
141	        const val = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? -1 : ci;
142	        for (const [qx, qy] of pointClusters[other]) idxMap[qx][qy] = val;
143	      };
144	      claim(px - 1, py); claim(px + 1, py); claim(px, py - 1); claim(px, py + 1);
145	    }
146	  }
147	  // 4) 重组成簇,<4 点丢弃;块坐标 ±0.25 抖动
148	  const clusters: Array<Array<[number, number]>> = pointClusters.map(() => []);
149	  for (let x = 0; x < gw; x++) {
150	    for (let y = 0; y < gh; y++) {
151	      if (idxMap[x][y] !== -1) clusters[idxMap[x][y]].push([x, y]);
152	    }
153	  }
154	  const finalClusters: Array<Array<[number, number]>> = [];
155	  for (const cl of clusters) {
156	    if (cl.length < 4) continue;
157	    finalClusters.push(cl.map(([bx, by]) => [
158	      bx + (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.5, by + (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.5,
159	    ] as [number, number]));
160	  }
161	  const hiveW = width, hiveH = hiveBottom - hiveTop;
162	  // ---- 场强分层(PlaceClustersArea):±10 窗口判簇(簇首块),簇内求和取前二大 ----
163	  for (let dx = -20; dx < hiveW + 20; dx++) {
164	    for (let dy = -20; dy < hiveH + 20; dy++) {
165	      const px = ox + dx, py = hiveTop + dy;
166	      if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
167	      let shellType: number = SAND;
168	      if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) shellType = HARDSAND;
169	      const vx = dx / cellW - 0.5, vy = dy / cellH - 0.5;
170	      let m1 = 0, m2 = 0, m1Idx = -1;
171	      for (let ci = 0; ci < finalClusters.length; ci++) {
172	        const c0 = finalClusters[ci][0];
173	        if (Math.abs(c0[0] - vx) > 10 || Math.abs(c0[1] - vy) > 10) continue;
174	        let f = 0;
175	        for (const [bx, by] of finalClusters[ci]) {
176	          const ddx = bx - vx, ddy = by - vy;
177	          f += 1 / (ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy);
178	        }
179	        if (f > m1) { if (m1 > m2) m2 = m1; m1 = f; m1Idx = ci; }
180	        else if (f > m2) m2 = f;
181	      }
182	      const field = m1 + m2;
183	      const ex = (dx / hiveW) * 2 - 1, ey = (dy / hiveH) * 2 - 1;
184	      const isEdge = Math.hypot(ex, ey) >= 0.8;
185	      const i = st.idx(px, py);
186	      if (field > 3.5) {
187	        // 腔室:清空 + 墙187;获胜簇索引 %15==2 → 化石(确定性判定)
188	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
189	        st.wall[i] = 187;
190	        if (m1Idx % 15 === 2) { st.type[i] = FOSSIL; st.flags[i] = 1; }
191	      } else if (field > 1.8) {
192	        // 隧道带:墙187 + 沙岩;地表以下注岩浆
193	        st.wall[i] = 187;
194	        if (py < gs.worldSurface) { st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0; }
195	        else st.liquidType[i] = 2;
196	        if (!isEdge || st.flags[i]) { st.type[i] = SANDSTONE; st.flags[i] = 1; }
197	      } else if (field > 0.7 || !isEdge) {
198	        // 硬化沙壳:墙216 + 沙/硬化沙(1/3)
199	        st.wall[i] = 216;
200	        st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
201	        if (!isEdge || st.flags[i]) { st.type[i] = shellType; st.flags[i] = 1; }
202	      } else if (field > 0.25 && rng.next() < (field - 0.25) / 0.45) {
203	        // 隧道外缘噪声
204	        st.wall[i] = 187;
205	        if (py < gs.worldSurface) { st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0; }
206	        else st.liquidType[i] = 2;
207	        if (st.flags[i]) { st.type[i] = shellType; st.flags[i] = 1; }
208	      }
209	    }
210	  }
211	  // ---- AddTileVariance:沙 53 下方 2 格非实心 → 酥化为 397(装饰 485/484/165/187 略)----
212	  for (let dx = -20; dx < width + 20; dx++) {
213	    for (let dy = -20; dy < hiveH + 20; dy++) {
214	      const px = ox + dx, py = hiveTop + dy;
215	      if (!st.inBounds(px, py) || !st.inBounds(px, py + 2)) continue;
216	      const i = st.idx(px, py);
217	      if (st.type[i] === SAND && st.flags[i]
218	        && !(st.isSolid(px, py + 1) && st.isSolid(px, py + 2))) {
219	        st.type[i] = HARDSAND;
220	      }
221	    }
222	  }
223	
224	  // ---- 入口(33%):竖井通到蜂巢顶(原版四种入口的简化)----
225	  if (rng.next() <= 0.3333) {
226	    const ex = ox + rng.int(Math.floor(width * 0.2), Math.floor(width * 0.8));
227	    let ey = 0;
228	    while (ey < h - 2 && !st.flags[st.idx(ex, ey)]) ey++;
229	    const shaftW = rng.int(0, 3) === 0 ? 8 : 4;
230	    for (let y = ey - 2; y <= hiveTop + 8; y++) {
231	      for (let dx = 0; dx < shaftW; dx++) {
232	        if (!st.inBounds(ex + dx, y)) continue;
233	        const i = st.idx(ex + dx, y);
234	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
235	        st.wall[i] = 187;
236	      }
237	    }
238	  }
239	
240	  gs.undergroundDesert = {
241	    x0: Math.max(0, ox - 10), y0: Math.max(0, topY - 10),
242	    x1: Math.min(w, ox + width + 10), y1: Math.min(h, hiveBottom + 10),
243	  };
244	}
245	
246	function rowIsValid(st: TileStore, x: number, factor: number): boolean {
247	  const width = Math.floor(320 * factor);
248	  const x0 = Math.max(1, x - Math.floor(width / 2));
249	  let y = 0;
250	  while (y < st.h - 2 && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) y++;
251	  y = Math.min(st.h - 2, y + 2);
252	  for (let i = x0; i < Math.min(st.w - 1, x0 + width); i++) {
253	    const t = st.type[st.idx(i, y)];
254	    if (t === MUD || t === JUNGLE_GRASS || t === SNOW || t === ICE) return false;
255	  }
256	  return true;
257	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-10T02:17:28.405Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
TS settle: quickWater(3,-1) default ✓ same as QuickWater(3,-1,-1); waterCheck ✓; quickSettle loop until numLiquid==0 (original: 10 outer iterations each running until numLiquid==0 — i.e., original re-runs 10 times; TS runs once until convergence). Original runs the whole settle 10 times (outer while num2<10) — this catches late-arriving liquid. TS does it once. Difference: minor, but if some liquid never settles TS stops at MAX_ITER; original does 10 passes. Practically the settled result is similar.

However there's a big ordering difference: original settles liquids at pass 49 (mid-generation, after lakes/hell/etc., before smooth world), and again at pass 97 (near end, after plants). TS settles once after ALL generation, in Game.newWorld. Net effect similar, but TS's own `liquidPass` adds lakes/pools with legacy logic (local-minima lakes 10-26 wide, 60 random underground pools 5-14 wide) that are NOT in vanilla (vanilla has "Lakes" pass L6448 — did TS port Lakes? No; legacy liquidPass instead). This is a shape difference: TS lakes are placed by surface-local-minima detection with random widths; vanilla Lakes pass uses different algorithm. I should note it briefly.

Now I have plenty. Time to write the structured comparison. Also should double check the TS TerrainPass bug: `rightBeach = w - gs.beachRightStart`. So gs.beachRightStart is an X coordinate (right beach start column). Looking at pass list: `...(useVanillaTerrain ? [...原版地形, 洞穴, 海滩, 生物群系, 清理, 生命水晶] : ...)`, then orePass, liquidPass, halfbrick..., hellPass, hell houses, potPass, { name: '表面', run: surfacePass } — always! then vanillaSurfaceDecor if vanilla. So both surfacePass and vanillaSurfaceDecor run → two tree passes. surfacePass plants trees on GRASS with spacing 6-14; then vanillaSurfaceDecor plants again with spacing 6-14 (jungle 5-10). So trees may be denser or conflicting (vanillaSurfaceDecor skips columns where flags exist above). Actually both plant T.TREE; second pass checks `!st.flags[sy-1]` so it won't overplant on top of existing trunk bases... it scans the first solid tile of the column which after a tree is planted is the grass below trunk; sy-1 is the trunk tile → flags set → skip tree, but then grass/flower check also requires !flags[sy-1] → falls through. So double-pass mostly redundant but the second pass adds jungle/cactus/vine decorations. Mention briefly.

| TuneOceanDepth | TS:61-101:16 档阈值,**按 `van=w/4200` 缩放**(t(3)/t(6)/…/t(255)) | L11682-11752:阈值为**绝对列数** 3/6/9/15/50/…/255,不缩放 | ✗ 大世界 ✓;中小世界海盆斜坡曲线提前变陡(代码注释已声明是刻意缩放) |
| 盆缘 | TS:109、129/156:`edgeWall=round(30·van)` | L6671、6721:固定 `>30 / <maxTilesX-30` | ✗ 同上,仅中小世界 |
| 挖盆/灌水/铺沙 | TS:133-143、160-170:水线 surfY+int(1,4)、水柱 `y<waterY+depth*0.75-3`、y==waterY 液量 127、盆壁沙、清墙 | L6679-6700、6719-6750 同 | ✓ |

## 5. 出生点(pass 78)/ 树木(pass 82)

### 5a. Spawn Point
我方 `WorldGen.ts:843-907(finalize)`;原版 L9469-9508。

- 原版:从 `w/2` 起以 `Next(-n,n+1)` 扩张窗口,取首个实心格;若 spawnY>worldSurface 或头顶有液体则 n++ 重试;**不改地形**。
- 我方:中心 ±60 列内找 9 列窗口方差最小处,再把 ±5 列**强制整平**到平均高度(填/挖/铺草/清树)。
- ✗ **我方出生点是一块人工平台**,原版是自然地表;且原版逐次扩张的随机采样被我方换成了确定性的"最平坦窗口"。

### 5b. Planting Trees
我方两条路径都在跑:`WorldGen.ts:695-776(旧 surfacePass)` + `StructuresPass.ts:225-301(runSurfaceDecorPass)`;原版 L9651-9669(Planting Trees)→ AddTrees L15642-15675 → GrowTree L14171。

| 项 | 我方 | 原版 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 种植密度 | 两个 pass 都是"步进 int(6,14)"(丛林 int(5,10)),即平均间距 ~10 列 | AddTrees **逐列尝试**(L15646),仅 `Next(3)==0`/`Next(4)==0` 跳列 → 平均尝试间距 ~1.6 列,成活率由 GrowTree 空位检查限制 | ✗ **我方森林稀疏数倍**——原版森林连成片,我方是零星单株 |
| 树高 | int(7,14)(丛林 8-16) | GrowTree L14180:`Next(5,17)`=5-16,净空要求 num2+4(丛林 +5) | ✗ 高度分布不同(偏窄偏高) |
| 树枝 | 2-4 枝、每位置 40%、上限 | GrowTree 帧逻辑 num5∈0-9 带去重约束 | ≈(形状近似) |
| 棕榈树 | 无 | L15650-15656:外侧 380 列、概率 0.10-0.45 逐列 GrowPalmTree | ✗ 海滩无棕榈 |
| 巨型树(EpicTree) | 无 | L9656-9666:`w*0.003` 个 25-50 宽林区尝试 | ✗ |
| 观赏树(樱/柳) | 无 | L15660-15665:草上 1/20 | ✗(小) |

## 6. Liquids 沉降初始状态(pass 49 / 97)

我方 `Game.ts:282-318(settleLiquids)` + `LiquidSim.ts`;原版 L7395-7432(pass 49 "Settle Liquids")与 L10247+(pass 97 "Settle Liquids Again")。

| 项 | 我方 | 原版 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|---|
| QuickWater / WaterCheck | Game.ts:299-302:`quickWater()`(默认 minY=3,maxY=-1)→ `waterCheck()` | `Liquid.QuickWater(3,-1,-1)` + `WaterCheck()` | ✓ |
| 沉降循环 | Game.ts:303-314:quickSettle=true,**单轮** `updateLiquid()` 直到 numLiquid==0(MAX_ITER=100000) | 外层 `while(num2<10)` 共 **10 轮**,每轮跑到 numLiquid==0 后再 WaterCheck | ✗ 我方只沉降 1 遍;原版 10 遍可捕获晚到的液体(实际收敛结果多数一致,但边界水洼可能残留) |
| 时机 | **全部 pass 跑完后**(newWorld 内)一次性沉降 | pass 49(生成中段,Lakes 之后)+ pass 97(收尾,植物之后)两次 | ✗ 时序不同;影响小 |
| 初始水体来源 | `WorldGen.ts:646-692(旧 liquidPass)`:地表局部极小值灌湖(宽 10-26)+ 60 个随机地下水池(5-14×2-4) | Lakes pass L6448-6564:`Next(3,6)·w/4200` 个,避海滩/湖/山洞/隧道 ±150/±100,从 worldSurfaceLow-20 向下找腔 | ✗ 湖的数量与位置算法不同(我方按"局部洼地"探测,原版按拒绝采样 + 腔体判定) |

---

## 汇总:肉眼可见的世界形态差异(按影响排序)

1. **矿石(pass 27)完全未移植**——仍在用旧 `orePass`:数量固定不随面积、深度三带丢失、脉形不同、**邪恶矿缺失**。(`WorldGen.ts:605-643` vs `WorldGen.cs:5837-5922`)
2. **Mount Caves(L5091)与 Mountain Caves(pass 34)缺失**——世界没有原版的随机山丘,山侧大洞口(Cavinator/CaveOpenater)也没有。(全仓库无对应实现)
3. **树木密度差数倍**——原版逐列尝试(间距 ~1.6 列),我方步进 6-14 列;且无棕榈/巨型树。(`StructuresPass.ts:230`、`WorldGen.ts:723` vs `WorldGen.cs:15642-15675`)
4. **丛林巨型泥 runner 漏传 `noYChange:true`**——vy 从 -20 被钳到 ±1,泥柱高度只有原版 ~1/20 速率的累积;另 TileRunner 缺 type-59 泥专用 vy 钳制(`TileRunner.ts:170` 缺 `WorldGen.cs:46681-46691`)。
5. **浮空岛数量少 + 无天湖**——`floor(w*0.0008)` vs 原版再加 skyLakes(大世界 3 vs 6),且无 CloudLake。(`StructuresPass.ts:27` vs `WorldGen.cs:5557+4715-4719`)
6. **沙漠蜂巢深度系数错误**——`(rng.next()+1)` 应为 `(rng.next()*0.5+1.5)`,range 170-340 vs 255-340。(`DesertPass.ts:37` vs `DesertDescription.cs:53`)
7. **出生点人工整平平台** vs 原版自然地表采样。(`WorldGen.ts:851-881` vs `WorldGen.cs:9469-9508`)
8. **泥土层/岩石层洞穴(pass 13/14)用 digTunnel 替代 TileRunner**——洞形从直线变蜿蜒(代码注释为有意为之)。
9. **沉降只做 1 遍 + 一次性后置**,原版 10 遍 × 两次;湖泊初始来源算法不同。(`Game.ts:296-318` vs `WorldGen.cs:7395-7432/10247`)
10. 小世界特有:TerrainPass 缺 `num10+=0.02` 钳位;Beach 的 TuneOceanDepth 阈值与盆缘 30 被按 `w/4200` 缩放(刻意)。

**一致性最好的部分**:TerrainPass 核心游走、TileRunner 本体、Small Holes/Surface Caves/Caverer、地狱(Underworld)、雪原(Ice Biome)、Ocean Sand、海滩大世界曲线、沙丘剖面(SandMound)、丛林隧道与收尾。
```
